📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • MPV 刺激导致免疫可溶性介质水平发生显着变化,包括对 抗病毒药物 防御和免疫调节至关重要的趋化因子和细胞因子的减少。治疗 与 3F2 联合使用可显着提高 CCL2、CXCL8、IFN-γ、IL-12(p40)、IL-15、IL-5 和必需生长因子(如 VEGF 和 NGF-β)的水平。
  • 在确诊的 猴痘 病例(G1)中,感染集中在男性(97.7%)、19至59岁的成年人(98.8%)、贝伦居民(76.7%)、受过高等教育的人(53.5%)以及自称同性恋(73.3%)或双性恋(12.8%)取向的人中。发热(81.4%)和局部生殖器病变(G1 为 43.0%,G2 为 6.2%)是主要临床表现。亲密性接触是主要的暴露途径(65.1%)。在 G1 期观察到 HIV 合并感染的比例很高(54.7%),其中 23.3% 的人在接到通知时表现出活动性 STI,主要是梅毒(16.3%)。临床治疗以门诊为主,住院率7.0%,有效确诊病例治愈率96.5%。
  • 这些 hKG 的功能富集分析揭示了 猴痘 感染的关键致病过程,包括生物过程、分子功能、细胞成分和 KEGG 途径。此外,前三种复合物(ALB-特考韦瑞、CDH1-Maraviroc 和 PPARG-Beclabuvir)的 100 ns 分子动力学 (MD) 模拟显示出强大的结合稳定性,并得到 RMSD、RMSF 和 MM-GBSA 结果的支持。
  • 流行病学数据显示,人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)、沙眼衣原体、单纯疱疹病毒、梅毒等性传播感染经常与 猴痘 感染相关,这凸显了性接触在 猴痘 感染中的作用。 [10.1097/olq.0000000000002438]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • MPV stimulation led to significant changes in immunosoluble mediator levels, including reductions in chemokines and cytokines crucial for antiviral defense and immune regulation. [10.1007/s00011-026-02387-3].
  • In confirmed Mpox cases (G1), infection was concentrated among men (97.7%), adults aged 19 to 59 years (98.8%), residents of Belém (76.7%), individuals with tertiary education (53.5%), and those reporting homosexual (73.3%) or bisexual (12.8%) orientation.
  • Functional enrichment analysis of these hKGs revealed critical pathogenic processes of MPOX infection, including biological processes, molecular functions, cellular components, and KEGG pathways.
  • Epidemiological data reveal that STIs such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), Chlamydia trachomatis, herpes simplex virus, syphilis have been frequently associated with mpox infection highlighting the role of sexual contact in its transmission.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 据南非政府通讯社报道,南非发现了四起新的 猴痘 病例。 [news-a5e3cf968ea7280a]。
  • 猴痘 暴发,包括进化枝 I 和进化枝 II,正在进行中。最近的暴发主要通过亲密或性接触传播,有些病例涉及家庭传播。CDC 报告称,进化枝 I 暴发 已归属于 Ib 亚进化枝,而进化枝 II 暴发 自 2022 年以来一直在持续存在。
  • 自 2023 年底以来,I 型猴痘 暴发 已在中非和东非发生,西欧国家较新的 暴发 可能与正在发生的 暴发 有关。在美国,自 2026 年 3 月以来,分支 I 猴痘病例有所增加,在多个司法管辖区观察到持续分支 Ib 传播 的转变。
  • 2026 年 9 月 16 日,开赛省宣布了 猴痘 暴发。截至 2026 年 10 月 1 日,所有 18 个卫生区报告了 1,017 例病例和超过 24 例死亡。据报道,暴发 仍在进行中,国家卫生当局于 2026 年 10 月 4 日向奇卡帕部署了快速干预小组。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • Four new mpox cases identified in South Africa, reported by the South African Government News Agency. [news-a5e3cf968ea7280a].
  • Monkeypox outbreaks, including both clade I and clade II, are ongoing. Recent outbreaks have primarily spread through intimate or sexual contact, with some cases involving household transmission. [news-a0ad317dd700afa5].
  • Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox have occurred in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with newer outbreaks in Western European countries likely related to this ongoing outbreak. [news-b5c65cab18ffd6be].
  • A mpox outbreak was declared in Kasaï province on 16 September 2026. By 1 October 2026, 1,017 cases and more than 24 deaths had been reported across all 18 health zones.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Infection  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-16  |  DOI: 10.1007/s15010-026-03022-3  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

巴西亚马逊 猴痘 的流行病学和社会人口学概况:一项横断面研究

Epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of Mpox in Brazilian Amazon: a cross-sectional study
作者： Gabriel Palheta Beltrão, Marcos Vinicius Neves dos Santos, Leonardo Miranda dos Santos, Victor Augusto de Alencar Menezes, Silvio Augusto Fernandes de Menezes, Ricardo Roberto de Souza Fonseca, Luiz Fernando Almeida Machado
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘 暴发 的特点是性网络内的病毒 传播 和非典型的临床表现,需要区域流行病学评估。本研究旨在描述巴西亚马逊帕拉州(2022-2025 年)疑似和确诊 猴痘 病例的社会人口统计学特征、时间通知趋势和临床特征。材料和方法:2022 年 3 月至 2025 年 12 月期间基于法定疾病信息系统 (SINAN) 的观察性、横断面和分析研究,遵循 STROBE 指南。样本 (N = 296) 分为确诊 猴痘 病例 (G1;n = 86) 和排除病例 (G2;n = 210)。使用 Pearson 卡方检验(对于预期细胞计数 ≥ 5)或 Fisher 精确检验(对于预期计数 < 5)评估关联性,在 SPSS v.26.0 中将显着性设置为 p < 0.05。结果:在 G1 中,感染集中在男性 (97.7%)、顺性别男性 (96.5%)、19 至 59 岁的成年人 (98.8%)、贝伦居民 (76.7%)、受过高等教育的个人 (53.5%) 以及同性恋 (73.3%) 或双性恋 (12.8%) 取向的人 (p < 0.001)。临床上,发热(81.4%;p < 0.001)和局部生殖器病变(G1 为 43.0%,G2 为 6.2%;p < 0.001)是主要表现。亲密性接触是主要的可能暴露途径(65.1%;p < 0.001)。在 G1 期观察到 HIV 合并感染的比例很高 (54.7%),报告与免疫抑制状态完全重叠,同时 23.3% 的人在收到通知时出现活动性 STI (p < 0.001),主要是活动性梅毒 (16.3%)。临床治疗主要为门诊治疗(住院率为 7.0%;p = 0.261),有效确诊病例的治愈率为 96.5%。确诊病例中没有发生 猴痘 归因的死亡,但 G1 中全因 猴痘 的死亡率为 2.3% (n = 2)。结论:帕拉州的 猴痘 病例集中在州首府,与年轻人之间的亲密性接触网络有关,其特点是 HIV 和梅毒合并感染频率较高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要了解巴西亚马逊地区 猴痘 的流行病学和社会人口学概况,因为 暴发 显示性网络中的病毒 传播 和非典型的临床表现。该研究旨在描述该地区 猴痘 病例的人口统计学和临床​​特征。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究使用了 2022 年 3 月至 2025 年 12 月期间来自法定疾病信息系统 (SINAN) 的数据。样本包括 296 例病例,分为确诊 猴痘 病例(G1;n = 86)和排除病例(G2;n = 210)。
核心方法
该研究是观察性和分析性的,遵循 STROBE 指南。使用 Pearson 卡方检验和 Fisher 精确检验等统计检验评估关联性,在 SPSS v.26.0 中将显着性设置为 p < 0.05。
主要结果
在确诊的 猴痘 病例(G1)中,感染集中在男性(97.7%)、19至59岁的成年人(98.8%)、贝伦居民(76.7%)、受过高等教育的人(53.5%)以及自称同性恋(73.3%)或双性恋(12.8%)取向的人中。发热(81.4%)和局部生殖器病变(G1 为 43.0%,G2 为 6.2%)是主要临床表现。亲密性接触是主要的暴露途径(65.1%)。在 G1 期观察到 HIV 合并感染的比例很高(54.7%),其中 23.3% 的人在接到通知时表现出活动性 STI,主要是梅毒(16.3%)。临床治疗以门诊为主,住院率7.0%,有效确诊病例治愈率96.5%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,帕拉州的 猴痘 病例集中在州首府,与年轻人之间的亲密性接触网络有关。他们注意到 HIV 和梅毒的双重感染频率很高,强调了 猴痘 与该人群中这些疾病之间的新关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为巴西亚马逊地区的针对性 监测 和预防策略提供了信息,特别关注高风险群体,例如男男性行为者和艾滋病毒感染者。他们还强调需要采取综合方法来解决同时发生的性传播感染问题。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于横截面设计,这限制了因果推断。样本量相对较小,且数据来源于通知系统,可能会引入报告偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of Mpox in Brazilian Amazon: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Gabriel Palheta Beltrão, Marcos Vinicius Neves dos Santos, Leonardo Miranda dos Santos, Victor Augusto de Alencar Menezes, Silvio Augusto Fernandes de Menezes, Ricardo Roberto de Souza Fonseca, Luiz Fernando Almeida Machado
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The Mpox outbreak was characterized by viral transmission within sexual networks and atypical clinical presentations, requiring regional epidemiological evaluations. This study aimed to describe the sociodemographic profile, temporal notification trends, and clinical characteristics of suspected and confirmed Mpox cases in Pará, Brazilian Amazon (2022-2025). MATERIALS AND METHODS: Observational, cross-sectional, and analytical study based on the Notifiable Diseases Information System (SINAN) between March 2022 and December 2025, adhering to STROBE guidelines. The sample (N = 296) was stratified into confirmed Mpox cases (G1; n = 86) and ruled-out cases (G2; n = 210). Associations were evaluated using Pearson's Chi-square test (for expected cell counts ≥ 5) or Fisher's exact test (for expected counts < 5), with significance set at p < 0.05 in SPSS v.26.0. RESULTS: In G1, infection was concentrated among men (97.7%), cisgender men (96.5%), adults aged 19 to 59 years (98.8%), residents of Belém (76.7%), individuals with tertiary education (53.5%), and those reporting homosexual (73.3%) or bisexual (12.8%) orientation (p < 0.001). Clinically, fever (81.4%; p < 0.001) and localized genital lesions (43.0% in G1 vs. 6.2% in G2; p < 0.001) were the primary presentations. Intimate sexual contact was the primary probable exposure pathway (65.1%; p < 0.001). A high proportion of HIV coinfection was observed in G1 (54.7%), with complete reporting overlap with immunosuppression status, alongside 23.3% presenting an active STI at notification (p < 0.001), predominantly active syphilis (16.3%). Clinical management was predominantly outpatient (7.0% hospitalization rate; p = 0.261), with a 96.5% cure rate among valid confirmed cases. No Mpox-attributed deaths occurred among confirmed cases, though all-cause mortality in G1 was 2.3% (n = 2). CONCLUSION: Mpox cases in Pará were concentrated in the state capital, associated with intimate sexual contact networks among young adults, and characterized by high coinfection frequencies with HIV and syphilis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to understand the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of Mpox in the Brazilian Amazon, as the outbreak showed viral transmission within sexual networks and atypical clinical presentations. The research aimed to characterize the demographic and clinical features of Mpox cases in this region.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study used data from the Notifiable Diseases Information System (SINAN) between March 2022 and December 2025. The sample included 296 cases, divided into confirmed Mpox cases (G1; n = 86) and ruled-out cases (G2; n = 210).
Core methods
The study was observational and analytical, following STROBE guidelines. Associations were evaluated using statistical tests such as Pearson's Chi-square test and Fisher's exact test, with significance set at p < 0.05 in SPSS v.26.0.
Main results
In confirmed Mpox cases (G1), infection was concentrated among men (97.7%), adults aged 19 to 59 years (98.8%), residents of Belém (76.7%), individuals with tertiary education (53.5%), and those reporting homosexual (73.3%) or bisexual (12.8%) orientation. Fever (81.4%) and localized genital lesions (43.0% in G1 vs. 6.2% in G2) were the primary clinical presentations. Intimate sexual contact was the main exposure pathway (65.1%). A high proportion of HIV coinfection was observed in G1 (54.7%), with 23.3% presenting an active STI at notification, predominantly syphilis (16.3%). Clinical management was predominantly outpatient, with a 7.0% hospitalization rate and a 96.5% cure rate among valid confirmed cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that Mpox cases in Pará were concentrated in the state capital and associated with intimate sexual contact networks among young adults. They noted the high coinfection frequencies with HIV and syphilis, highlighting a novel association between Mpox and these conditions in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform targeted surveillance and prevention strategies in the Brazilian Amazon, particularly focusing on high-risk groups such as men who have sex with men and individuals with HIV. They also emphasize the need for integrated approaches to address co-occurring STIs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a cross-sectional design, which limits causal inference. The sample size is relatively small, and the data are sourced from a notification system, which may introduce reporting biases. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

Journal: PLOS Neglected Tropical Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-16  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pntd.0014797  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 20, 10, e0014797
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

通过 宿主 转录组分析揭示猴痘 (猴痘) 的致病过程和药物再利用

Disclosing pathogenic processes of monkeypox (MPOX) and drug repurposing through host-transcriptomics analysis
作者： Sabkat Mahmud, Alvira Ajadee, Md. Ahad Ali, Md. Feroj Ahmed, Reaz Ahmmed, Md. Masud Rana, Tasfia Noor, Md. Fahim Faysal, Md. Hadiul Kabir, Md. Nurul Haque Mollah
摘要中文翻译2022 年非洲发生的猴痘(猴痘)病毒感染 暴发 最初被认为是一种被忽视的热带病(NTD)。现在它被认为是一个全球性的健康问题。最近的全球蔓延强调了需要加大力度打击 猴痘。尽管存在一些针对 猴痘 的 疫苗,但全世界大多数人由于不了解或无法获得疫苗,仍然没有接种疫苗。然而,到目前为止,还没有针对猴痘的批准药物。因此,针对猴痘的有效药物也是必不可少的。本研究试图基于猴痘相关的宿主关键基因(hKGs)来揭示猴痘的致病过程和治疗策略。首先,分析了三个转录组图谱数据集,鉴定了 宿主 患者和对照组之间 166 个常见的 宿主 差异表达基因 (hDEG),其中包括 75 个上调 hDEG 和 91 个下调 hDEG。随后,通过蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用(PPI)网络分析,六个排名最高的 宿主 关键基因(hKG):ALB、HSP90AA1、CDH1、PTPRC、IGF1 和 ABL1 被鉴定为潜在的 宿主 致病基因。这些 hKG 的功能富集分析揭示了 猴痘 感染的关键致病过程,包括生物过程、分子功能、细胞成分和 KEGG 途径。涉及转录因子和 miRNA 的调控网络分析提供了对疾病机制的进一步见解。基于分子对接分析,五个排名靠前的药物分子 特考韦瑞、Beclabuvir、Maraviroc、Raltegravir 和 Brincidofovir 被确定为针对 猴痘 的潜在可重复利用疗法。ADME/T 和药物相似性评估证实了这些候选药物具有良好的药代动力学特性。此外,前三种复合物(ALB-特考韦瑞、CDH1-Maraviroc 和 PPARG-Beclabuvir)的 100 ns 分子动力学 (MD) 模拟显示出强大的结合稳定性,并得到 RMSD、RMSF 和 MM-GBSA 结果的支持。因此,本研究提供的结果可能是 诊断 和 猴痘 患者治疗的潜在资源。然而,该研究结果需要在湿实验室中进行实验验证才能进一步证实。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2022 年非洲发生的猴痘(猴痘)病毒感染 暴发 最初被认为是一种被忽视的热带病(NTD)。现在它被认为是一个全球性的健康问题。
设计与对象
本研究试图基于猴痘相关的宿主关键基因(hKGs)来揭示猴痘的致病过程和治疗策略。
核心方法
首先,分析了三个转录组图谱数据集,鉴定了 宿主 患者和对照组之间 166 个常见的 宿主 差异表达基因 (hDEG),其中包括 75 个上调 hDEG 和 91 个下调 hDEG。
主要结果
这些 hKG 的功能富集分析揭示了 猴痘 感染的关键致病过程,包括生物过程、分子功能、细胞成分和 KEGG 途径。此外,前三种复合物(ALB-特考韦瑞、CDH1-Maraviroc 和 PPARG-Beclabuvir)的 100 ns 分子动力学 (MD) 模拟显示出强大的结合稳定性,并得到 RMSD、RMSF 和 MM-GBSA 结果的支持。
解释与创新
基于分子对接分析,五个排名靠前的药物分子 特考韦瑞、Beclabuvir、Maraviroc、Raltegravir 和 Brincidofovir 被确定为针对 猴痘 的潜在可重复利用疗法。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提供的结果可能是 诊断 和 猴痘 患者治疗的潜在资源。
局限与证据强度
然而,该研究结果需要在湿实验室中进行实验验证才能进一步证实。

Disclosing pathogenic processes of monkeypox (MPOX) and drug repurposing through host-transcriptomics analysis

Authors: Sabkat Mahmud, Alvira Ajadee, Md. Ahad Ali, Md. Feroj Ahmed, Reaz Ahmmed, Md. Masud Rana, Tasfia Noor, Md. Fahim Faysal, Md. Hadiul Kabir, Md. Nurul Haque Mollah
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox (MPOX) virus infection outbreak in Africa in 2022 was initially considered a neglected tropical disease (NTD). Now it is recognized as a global health problem. The recent global spread has emphasized the need for increased attention to combat MPOX. Although there are some vaccines against MPOX, most people worldwide are still not vaccinated due to their unawareness or unavailability. However, so far, there is no approved drug against MPOX yet. Therefore, effective drugs against MPOX are also essential. This study attempted to disclose pathogenic processes and therapeutic strategies of MPOX based on MPOX-associated host key-genes (hKGs). At first, three transcriptomic profile datasets were analyzed, identifying 166 common host differentially expressed genes (hDEGs) between MPOX patients and control groups, comprising 75 upregulated and 91 downregulated hDEGs. Subsequently, six top-ranked host key genes (hKGs): ALB, HSP90AA1, CDH1, PTPRC, IGF1, and ABL1, were identified as potential MPOX-causing genes through protein-protein interaction (PPI) network analysis. Functional enrichment analysis of these hKGs revealed critical pathogenic processes of MPOX infection, including biological processes, molecular functions, cellular components, and KEGG pathways. Regulatory network analysis involving transcription factors and miRNAs provided further insights into disease mechanisms. Based on molecular docking analysis, five top-ranked drug molecules Tecovirimat, Beclabuvir, Maraviroc, Raltegravir, and Brincidofovir were identified as potential repurposable therapeutics against MPOX. ADME/T and drug-likeness evaluations confirmed favorable pharmacokinetic properties for these candidates. Additionally, 100 ns molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of the top three complexes (ALB-Tecovirimat, CDH1-Maraviroc, and PPARG-Beclabuvir) demonstrated strong binding stability, supported by RMSD, RMSF, and MM-GBSA results. Therefore, the results provided in this study might be potential resources for diagnosis and therapies of MPOX patients. However, the findings require experimental validation in wet lab for further confirmation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox (MPOX) virus infection outbreak in Africa in 2022 was initially considered a neglected tropical disease (NTD). Now it is recognized as a global health problem.
Design and population
This study attempted to disclose pathogenic processes and therapeutic strategies of MPOX based on MPOX-associated host key-genes (hKGs).
Core methods
At first, three transcriptomic profile datasets were analyzed, identifying 166 common host differentially expressed genes (hDEGs) between MPOX patients and control groups, comprising 75 upregulated and 91 downregulated hDEGs.
Main results
Functional enrichment analysis of these hKGs revealed critical pathogenic processes of MPOX infection, including biological processes, molecular functions, cellular components, and KEGG pathways. Additionally, 100 ns molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of the top three complexes (ALB-Tecovirimat, CDH1-Maraviroc, and PPARG-Beclabuvir) demonstrated strong binding stability, supported by RMSD, RMSF, and MM-GBSA results.
Interpretation and novelty
Based on molecular docking analysis, five top-ranked drug molecules Tecovirimat, Beclabuvir, Maraviroc, Raltegravir, and Brincidofovir were identified as potential repurposable therapeutics against MPOX.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provided in this study might be potential resources for diagnosis and therapies of MPOX patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, the findings require experimental validation in wet lab for further confirmation.

分子 流行病学 在 暴发 场景中破译 猴痘 的 传播 网络:2023 年全球卫生紧急情况期间在泰国的应用。

Molecular Epidemiology to Decipher the Transmission Networks of Mpox in an Outbreak Scenario: Applications in Thailand During the 2023 Global Health Emergency.
作者： Spencer L Sterling, Kwankamon Rattanathumi, Thitipong Yingyong, Ananporn Supataragul, Nattakarn Thippamom, Tannawut Srisuk, Patchara Panyaratanaamporn, Chessadaporn Ngambunchuy, Panukon Rakklin, Thaniya Suwanmalai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2022 年 MPXV 全球卫生紧急事件强调了针对 猴痘 进化枝 IIb 暴发 采取快速诊断措施的必要性。
设计与对象
Within the observed substitutions, a majority of mutations were either G→A (n = 289, 49.74%) or C→T (n = 221, 38.04%), indicating APOBEC-3-driven deaminase activity.
核心方法
利用对 162 名 猴痘 感染者进行的靶向下一代测序,我们通过分子时间距离矩阵链接高相似性序列,创建了 猴痘 网络图。

我们的研究结果表明,研究人群与 传播 的全球趋势一致,几乎完全在男男性行为者群体中,大多数病例由 C 谱系病毒以及其他 8 个亚谱系病毒引起。

Molecular Epidemiology to Decipher the Transmission Networks of Mpox in an Outbreak Scenario: Applications in Thailand During the 2023 Global Health Emergency.

Authors: Spencer L Sterling, Kwankamon Rattanathumi, Thitipong Yingyong, Ananporn Supataragul, Nattakarn Thippamom, Tannawut Srisuk, Patchara Panyaratanaamporn, Chessadaporn Ngambunchuy, Panukon Rakklin, Thaniya Suwanmalai
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The 2022 MPXV global health emergency highlighted the necessity for rapid diagnostic measures in response to mpox clade IIb outbreaks. This study investigates the mpox lineages identified in Thailand during this period and explores the transmission dynamics of dominant lineages. METHODS: Using targeted next-generation sequencing from 162 individuals infected with mpox, we created a transmission network map by linking high-similarity sequences through molecular-temporal distance matrices. RESULTS: Our findings reveal that the study population aligns with global trends of transmission almost exclusively within the community of men who have sex with men, with a majority of cases resulting from lineage C viruses as well as 8 additional sublineages. Within the observed substitutions, a majority of mutations were either G→A (n = 289, 49.74%) or C→T (n = 221, 38.04%), indicating APOBEC-3-driven deaminase activity. We also present a global transmission network for lineage C mpox genomes, illustrating domestic and international spread. CONCLUSIONS: This research underscores the significance of whole genome sequencing in diagnostics during outbreaks and the importance of sharing genomic data to enhance our understanding of disease transmission on a global scale.

Core methods

Main results

氨基金刚烷衍生物 3F2 调节单核细胞和 NK 细胞活化并恢复因猴痘病毒刺激而改变的免疫介质

The aminoadamantane derivative 3F2 modulates monocyte and NK cell activation and restores immune mediators altered by Monkeypox virus stimuli
作者： Thaís de Fátima da Silva Moraes, Geovane Marques-Ferreira, Ágata Lopes-Ribeiro, Erik Vinicius de Sousa Reis, Gabriela Lorrany Aparecida Azevedo, Victor de Melo Rocha, Marília Bueno da Silva Menegatto, Felipe Alves Clarindo, Caio Wilker-Teixeira, Laura Cardoso Corrêa-Dias
摘要中文翻译摘要 已知猴痘病毒 (MPV) 采用多种免疫逃避策略,损害 抗病毒药物 防御并破坏免疫稳态。在这里,我们对灭活 MPV 刺激诱导的 免疫应答 进行了表征,并评估了氨基金刚烷 (AMA) 及其衍生物 3F2 恢复免疫功能的能力。经 MPV 刺激的外周血单核细胞的一部分单核细胞的激活发生改变,同时自然杀伤 (NK) 和自然杀伤 T (NKT) 细胞的激活受到抑制。MPV 刺激还导致免疫可溶性介质水平发生显着变化,包括对 抗病毒药物 防御和免疫调节至关重要的趋化因子和细胞因子的减少。事实证明,治疗 联合 3F2 比 AMA 更有效,可显着提高 CCL2、CXCL8、IFN-γ、IL-12(p40)、IL-15、IL-5 以及必需生长因子(如 VEGF 和 NGF-β)的水平。此外,3F2 治疗 部分逆转单核细胞活化并重建 NK 和 NKT 细胞功能。目前的结果表明,3F2 可以控制炎症,同时保留 抗病毒药物 反应性。这些发现表明,灭活的 MPV 刺激可深刻调节先天免疫激活,并将 3F2 确定为一种有前途的免疫调节候选物,能够部分恢复病毒颗粒刺激改变的 免疫应答。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征 MPV 刺激诱导的 免疫应答 并评估 3F2 恢复免疫功能的能力。众所周知,猴痘病毒 (MPV) 采用多种免疫逃避策略,损害 抗病毒药物 防御并破坏免疫稳态。
设计与对象
该研究评估了用灭活 MPV 刺激的外周血单核细胞中的 免疫应答。该研究是在实验室环境中使用人类捐赠者的细胞进行的。
核心方法
用灭活的 MPV 刺激外周血单核细胞,然后暴露于氨基金刚烷 (AMA) 及其衍生物 3F2。评估了单核细胞、NK 和 NKT 细胞的激活状态,以及趋化因子和细胞因子等免疫可溶性介质的水平。
主要结果
MPV 刺激导致免疫可溶性介质水平发生显着变化,包括对 抗病毒药物 防御和免疫调节至关重要的趋化因子和细胞因子的减少。治疗 与 3F2 联合使用可显着提高 CCL2、CXCL8、IFN-γ、IL-12(p40)、IL-15、IL-5 和必需生长因子(如 VEGF 和 NGF-β)的水平。
解释与创新
作者解释说,事实证明,3F2 治疗 在恢复免疫功能方面比 AMA 更有效。这些发现表明,灭活的 MPV 刺激可深刻调节先天免疫激活,并将 3F2 确定为一种有前途的免疫调节候选物,能够部分恢复病毒颗粒刺激改变的 免疫应答。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,3F2 可能是调节 猴痘 疾病中 免疫应答 的潜在治疗剂。他们还强调了了解 MPV 感染背景下的免疫调节对于制定有针对性的干预措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到体外模型使用的限制,并且没有考虑体内效应。证据是强有力的,因为它直接比较了 3F2 和 AMA 对免疫介质和激活状态的影响,并具有清晰的数值结果。

The aminoadamantane derivative 3F2 modulates monocyte and NK cell activation and restores immune mediators altered by Monkeypox virus stimuli

Authors: Thaís de Fátima da Silva Moraes, Geovane Marques-Ferreira, Ágata Lopes-Ribeiro, Erik Vinicius de Sousa Reis, Gabriela Lorrany Aparecida Azevedo, Victor de Melo Rocha, Marília Bueno da Silva Menegatto, Felipe Alves Clarindo, Caio Wilker-Teixeira, Laura Cardoso Corrêa-Dias
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The monkeypox virus (MPV) is known to employ multiple immune evasion strategies that compromise antiviral defenses and disrupt immune homeostasis. Here, we characterized the immune responses induced by stimulation with inactivated MPV and evaluated the ability of aminoadamantane (AMA) and its derivative, 3F2, to restore immune function. Peripheral blood mononuclear cells that were stimulated with MPV exhibited altered activation in a subset of monocytes, along with suppressed activation of natural killer (NK) and natural killer T (NKT) cells. MPV stimulation also led to significant changes in the levels of immunosoluble mediators, including reductions in chemokines and cytokines that are crucial for antiviral defense and immune regulation. Treatment with 3F2 proved to be more effective than AMA, resulting in significantly higher levels of CCL2, CXCL8, IFN-γ, IL-12(p40), IL-15, IL-5, and essential growth factors such as VEGF and NGF-β. Furthermore, 3F2 treatment partially reverses monocyte activation and re-establishes NK and NKT cell function. The current results indicate that 3F2 can control inflammation while preserving antiviral responsiveness. These findings demonstrate that inactivated MPV stimuli profoundly modulates innate immune activation and identify 3F2 as a promising immunomodulatory candidate capable of partially restoring immune responses altered by viral particle stimulation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the immune responses induced by MPV stimulation and evaluate the ability of 3F2 to restore immune function. The monkeypox virus (MPV) is known to employ multiple immune evasion strategies that compromise antiviral defenses and disrupt immune homeostasis.
Design and population
The study evaluated immune responses in peripheral blood mononuclear cells stimulated with inactivated MPV. The research was conducted in a laboratory setting using cells from human donors.
Core methods
Peripheral blood mononuclear cells were stimulated with inactivated MPV and then exposed to aminoadamantane (AMA) and its derivative, 3F2. The activation status of monocytes, NK, and NKT cells was assessed, along with the levels of immunosoluble mediators such as chemokines and cytokines.
Main results
MPV stimulation led to significant changes in immunosoluble mediator levels, including reductions in chemokines and cytokines crucial for antiviral defense and immune regulation. Treatment with 3F2 resulted in significantly higher levels of CCL2, CXCL8, IFN-γ, IL-12(p40), IL-15, IL-5, and essential growth factors such as VEGF and NGF-β.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that 3F2 treatment proved to be more effective than AMA in restoring immune function. These findings demonstrate that inactivated MPV stimuli profoundly modulates innate immune activation and identify 3F2 as a promising immunomodulatory candidate capable of partially restoring immune responses altered by viral particle stimulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that 3F2 could be a potential therapeutic agent for modulating immune responses in mpox disease. They also highlight the importance of understanding immune modulation in the context of MPV infection for developing targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of in vitro models and does not account for in vivo effects. The evidence is strong as it directly compares the effects of 3F2 and AMA on immune mediators and activation states, with clear numerical results.

保护引导反向疫苗学设计抗猴痘病毒的广泛免疫原性多表位 疫苗

Conservation guided reverse vaccinology for the design of a broadly immunogenic multi-epitope vaccine against monkeypox virus
作者： Harshit Sajal, Aswin Mohan, Rajesh Raju, Anuroopa G. Nadh
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是由猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 引起的,继 暴发 超越其传统流行地区之后,已成为全球公共卫生问题。尽管现有的天花衍生 疫苗 提供了部分保护,但其局限性和人群免疫力下降突出表明需要更安全、更有针对性的 疫苗 策略。本研究旨在识别保守的抗原靶标,并使用保守引导的免疫信息学方法设计针对 MPXV 的多表位 疫苗 候选药物。对来自不同地理区域的 27 个完整 MPXV 基因组 (2001-2025) 的数据集进行了分析,以识别高度保守的病毒基因。保守的表面暴露蛋白优先用于表位预测,包括细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞、辅助 T 淋巴细胞和 B 细胞表位。根据结合亲和力、抗原性、免疫原性、毒性、过敏原性和人类序列同源性的缺乏来选择高置信度表位。精心设计了具有广泛人类白细胞抗原 (HLA) 结合的混杂表位,以最大限度地扩大人群覆盖范围。使用适当的接头和佐剂设计了三个多表位 疫苗 构建体。进行结构建模、与先天免疫受体的分子对接、免疫应答 模拟和密码子优化来评估构建体。预计所有构建体都是稳定的、抗原性的和非过敏性的。分子对接表明 疫苗 构建体 2 (VC2) 与 TLR2 和 TLR4-MD-2 复合物有很强的相互作用,实现了最高的结合亲和力(分别为 ΔG = − 19.6 和 − 27.8 kcal/mol)。免疫模拟预测了强烈的体液和细胞反应,包括强抗体产生和 VC2 的干扰素-γ 诱导。群体覆盖度分析估计全球覆盖度为 99.27%,密码子优化表明在大肠杆菌中有效表达。总体而言,VC2 成为一种有前途的多表位 疫苗 候选药物,具有强大的预测免疫原性和结构特性。虽然这些计算结果奠定了坚实的基础,但 VC2 需要进一步的实验验证和临床前开发,作为针对 MPXV 的有针对性的 疫苗 策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 是由猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 引起的,继 暴发 超越其传统流行地区之后,已成为全球公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
对来自不同地理区域的 27 个完整 MPXV 基因组 (2001-2025) 的数据集进行了分析,以识别高度保守的病毒基因。
核心方法
保守的表面暴露蛋白优先用于表位预测,包括细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞、辅助 T 淋巴细胞和 B 细胞表位。根据结合亲和力、抗原性、免疫原性、毒性、过敏原性和人类序列同源性的缺乏来选择高置信度表位。精心设计了具有广泛人类白细胞抗原 (HLA) 结合的混杂表位,以最大限度地扩大人群覆盖范围。
主要结果
使用适当的接头和佐剂设计了三个多表位 疫苗 构建体。进行结构建模、与先天免疫受体的分子对接、免疫应答 模拟和密码子优化来评估构建体。预计所有构建体都是稳定的、抗原性的和非过敏性的。
解释与创新
疫苗 构建体 2 (VC2) 预计与 TLR2 和 TLR4-MD-2 复合物具有最强的相互作用,实现最高的结合亲和力(分别为 ΔG = -19.6 和 -27.8 kcal/mol)。免疫模拟预测了强烈的体液和细胞反应,包括强抗体产生和 VC2 的干扰素-γ 诱导。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发具有针对 MPXV 的广泛免疫原性潜力的多表位 疫苗 候选药物奠定了基础,这可以增强全球针对 猴痘 暴发 的准备。
局限与证据强度
虽然这些计算结果奠定了坚实的基础,但 VC2 需要进一步的实验验证和临床前开发,作为针对 MPXV 的有针对性的 疫苗 策略。

Conservation guided reverse vaccinology for the design of a broadly immunogenic multi-epitope vaccine against monkeypox virus

Authors: Harshit Sajal, Aswin Mohan, Rajesh Raju, Anuroopa G. Nadh
Original abstract (English)
Mpox, caused by the Monkeypox virus (MPXV), has emerged as a global public health concern following outbreaks beyond its traditional endemic regions. Although existing smallpox-derived vaccines provide partial protection, their limitations and declining population immunity highlight the need for safer and more targeted vaccine strategies. This study aimed to identify conserved antigenic targets and design a multi-epitope vaccine candidate against MPXV using a conservation-guided immunoinformatics approach. A dataset of 27 complete MPXV genomes (2001–2025) from diverse geographic regions was analyzed to identify highly conserved viral genes. Conserved surface-exposed proteins were prioritized for epitope prediction, including cytotoxic T-lymphocyte, helper T-lymphocyte, and B-cell epitopes. High-confidence epitopes were selected based on binding affinity, antigenicity, immunogenicity, toxicity, allergenicity, and absence of human sequence homology. Promiscuous epitopes with broad human leukocyte antigen (HLA) binding were curated to maximize population coverage. Three multi-epitope vaccine constructs were designed using appropriate linkers and adjuvants. Structural modeling, molecular docking with innate immune receptors, immune response simulation, and codon optimization were performed to evaluate the constructs. All constructs were predicted to be stable, antigenic, and non-allergenic. Molecular docking indicated strong interactions of vaccine construct 2 (VC2) with TLR2 and the TLR4–MD-2 complex, achieving the highest binding affinities (ΔG = − 19.6 and − 27.8 kcal/mol, respectively). Immune simulations predicted robust humoral and cellular responses, including strong antibody production and interferon-γ induction for VC2. Population coverage analysis estimated 99.27% global coverage, and codon optimization suggested efficient expression in Escherichia coli . Overall, VC2 emerges as a promising multi-epitope vaccine candidate with strong predicted immunogenic and structural properties. While these computational findings lay a solid foundation, VC2 require further experimental validation and preclinical development as a targeted vaccine strategy against MPXV.

Question and background
Mpox, caused by the Monkeypox virus (MPXV), has emerged as a global public health concern following outbreaks beyond its traditional endemic regions.

空气传播的猴痘病毒 传播 风险:具有感染能力的病毒的重要性。

Airborne transmission risk of monkeypox virus: the importance of infection-competent virus.
作者： Barry Atkinson, Susan Gould, Tom Fletcher, Jake Dunning, Dr Jake Dunning
摘要中文翻译陈兆良及其同事1提出,大量空气采样中定量PCR(qPCR)循环阈值(Ct)超过35不应等同于猴痘病毒的空气传播能力。尽管这种观点可能具有实际用途,但它并没有解决估计传播性的基本生物学基础:被检测的材料必须具有传染性,才有 传播 的潜力。我们认为,单独的 Ct 值不应被视为 传播 潜力的证据,因为 qPCR 无法区分来自具有感染能力的病毒体的核酸和来自不具有感染潜力的降解病毒体的核酸,因此不具有 传播 潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了了解估计猴痘病毒空气传播性的生物学基础的需要,强调 qPCR 循环阈值 (Ct) 值等当前方法可能无法准确反映 传播 潜力。
设计与对象
该研究是发表在《柳叶刀》上的一封信。微生物,重点关注猴痘病毒及其空气传播的 传播 风险,没有描述超出病毒一般背景的特定人群或环境。
核心方法
作者提出,大量空气采样中超过 35 的定量 PCR (qPCR) 循环阈值 (Ct) 值不应等同于猴痘病毒的空气传播能力,这突显了 qPCR 在区分传染性和非传染性病毒材料方面的局限性。
主要结果
该研究没有报告任何直接结果或数值结果,但提出了关于评估机载 传播 潜力时 qPCR Ct 值解释的关键点。
解释与创新
作者解释说,仅 qPCR 无法确定 传播 潜力,因为它无法区分具有感染能力的病毒体和降解的病毒体,并且他们强调需要更准确的生物学基础来估计传播性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,目前评估猴痘病毒空气传播风险的方法可能不足,这对公共卫生和风险评估策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于理论讨论,没有提供实证数据,导致基于缺乏直接实验证据的适度置信度评估。

Airborne transmission risk of monkeypox virus: the importance of infection-competent virus.

Authors: Barry Atkinson, Susan Gould, Tom Fletcher, Jake Dunning, Dr Jake Dunning
Original abstract (English)
Zhaoliang Chen and colleagues1 propose that quantitative PCR (qPCR) cycle threshold (Ct) values exceeding 35 in massive-volume air sampling should not be equated with airborne transmissibility for monkeypox virus. Although this opinion might have practical utility, it does not address the fundamental biological basis for estimating transmissibility: the material being detected must be infectious for there to be transmission potential. We argue that a Ct value alone should not be considered as evidence of transmission potential, as qPCR cannot distinguish between nucleic acid from infection-competent virions and nucleic acid from degraded virions that pose no infection potential and therefore no transmission potential.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand the biological basis for estimating airborne transmissibility of monkeypox virus, emphasizing that current methods like qPCR cycle threshold (Ct) values may not accurately reflect transmission potential.
Design and population
The study is a letter published in The Lancet. Microbe, focusing on monkeypox virus and its airborne transmission risk, with no specific population or setting described beyond the general context of the virus.
Core methods
The authors propose that quantitative PCR (qPCR) cycle threshold (Ct) values exceeding 35 in massive-volume air sampling should not be equated with airborne transmissibility for monkeypox virus, highlighting the limitations of qPCR in distinguishing between infectious and non-infectious viral material.
Main results
The study does not report any direct results or numerical findings but raises a critical point about the interpretation of qPCR Ct values in assessing airborne transmission potential.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that qPCR alone cannot determine transmission potential, as it cannot differentiate between infection-competent virions and degraded virions, and they emphasize the need for a more accurate biological basis for estimating transmissibility.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that current methods for assessing airborne transmission risk of monkeypox virus may be insufficient, which has implications for public health surveillance and risk assessment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a theoretical discussion and does not present empirical data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment based on the lack of direct experimental evidence.

孟加拉国有关 猴痘 的公众知识、态度和预防措施:基于社区的横断面调查的见解。

Public Knowledge, Attitudes, and Preventive Practices Regarding Mpox in Bangladesh: Insights From a Community-Based Cross-Sectional Survey.
作者： Hemayet Hossain, Md Hasan Ali, Md Abu Boccor Siddique, Sumaya Shargin Khan, Milon Mia, Suchona Akter, Afroza Sultana Nitu, Mohammad Nahian Rahman, Md Abdur Nur Sakib, Md Mosharof Hosen
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:猴痘 最近重新成为一个重大的全球健康问题,凸显了公众意识和预防措施的重要性。本研究旨在评估孟加拉国普通民众对 猴痘 及其 流行病学、预防和控制措施的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。方法:2024 年 6 月至 2024 年 9 月,采用遵循 STROBE 指南的多阶段随机抽样方法,对 16 个地区的 1002 名参与者进行了一项基于社区的横断面调查。通过结构化调查问卷收集数据,涵盖社会人口统计、知识、态度和实践。使用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析来确定社会人口因素与 KAP 结果之间的关联。结果:在 1002 名受访者中,只有 37.2% 的人表现出对 猴痘 的良好了解,而 74.0% 的人持积极态度,80.0% 的人表示令人满意的预防措施。对 猴痘 作为病毒性疾病的认识有限(37.3%),只有 22.5% 的人知道天花 疫苗接种 的保护作用。高等教育与良好知识(AOR = 11.8;95% CI:5.61-25.8)、积极态度(AOR = 5.68;95% CI:2.63-12.5)和令人满意的实践(AOR = 6.49;95% CI:2.62-16.8)密切相关。非边境居民更有可能报告积极态度(AOR = 3.07;p < 0.001)和令人满意的做法(AOR = 3.84;p < 0.001)。城市(AOR = 2.28;p = 0.003)和半城市(AOR = 2.19;p = 0.014)参与者表现出更积极的态度。态度和实践表现出最强的相关性(ρ = 0.793,p < 0.001),表明态度和预防实践之间存在很强的关联。结论:研究结果表明,意识和培训方面的差距与次优实践有关,突出了有针对性的教育干预的潜在领域。加强社区层面的认识和准备可能有助于孟加拉国为潜在的 猴痘 暴发 做好公共卫生准备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 最近重新成为全球重大健康问题,凸显了公众意识和预防措施的重要性。本研究旨在评估孟加拉国普通民众对 猴痘 及其 流行病学、预防和控制措施的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
2024 年 6 月至 2024 年 9 月,对 16 个地区的 1002 名参与者进行了一项基于社区的横断面调查。该研究主要针对孟加拉国的一般人群,采用遵循 STROBE 指南的多阶段随机抽样方法。
核心方法
通过结构化调查问卷收集数据,涵盖社会人口统计、知识、态度和实践。使用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析来确定社会人口因素与 KAP 结果之间的关联。
主要结果
在 1002 名受访者中,只有 37.2% 的人对 猴痘 表现出良好的了解,而 74.0% 的人持积极态度,80.0% 的人表示令人满意的预防措施。高等教育与良好知识(AOR = 11.8;95% CI:5.61-25.8)、积极态度(AOR = 5.68;95% CI:2.63-12.5)和令人满意的实践(AOR = 6.49;95% CI:2.62-16.8)密切相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,意识和培训方面的差距与次优实践有关,凸显了有针对性的教育干预的潜在领域。态度和实践表现出最强的相关性(ρ = 0.793,p < 0.001),表明态度和预防实践之间存在很强的关联。
科研与公卫意义
对 猴痘 作为病毒性疾病的认识有限(37.3%),只有 22.5% 的人知道天花 疫苗接种 的保护作用。加强社区层面的认识和准备可能有助于孟加拉国为潜在的 猴痘 暴发 做好公共卫生准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。证据是中等的,因为它基于自我报告的数据和关联,而不是直接的生物学或临床结果。

Public Knowledge, Attitudes, and Preventive Practices Regarding Mpox in Bangladesh: Insights From a Community-Based Cross-Sectional Survey.

Authors: Hemayet Hossain, Md Hasan Ali, Md Abu Boccor Siddique, Sumaya Shargin Khan, Milon Mia, Suchona Akter, Afroza Sultana Nitu, Mohammad Nahian Rahman, Md Abdur Nur Sakib, Md Mosharof Hosen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Mpox has recently re-emerged as a significant global health concern, highlighting the importance of public awareness and preventive measures. This study aimed to assess the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) regarding Mpox and its epidemiology, prevention, and control measures among the general population of Bangladesh. METHODS: A community-based cross-sectional survey was conducted from June 2024 to September 2024 among 1002 participants across 16 districts, using a multi-stage random sampling approach following STROBE guidelines. Data were collected through a structured questionnaire covering sociodemographics, knowledge, attitudes, and practices. Both univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were used to determine associations between sociodemographic factors and KAP outcomes. RESULTS: Out of 1002 respondents, only 37.2% demonstrated good knowledge of Mpox, while 74.0% had positive attitudes, and 80.0% reported satisfactory preventive practices. Awareness of Mpox as a viral disease was limited (37.3%), and only 22.5% knew about the protective role of smallpox vaccination. Higher education was strongly associated with good knowledge (AOR = 11.8; 95% CI: 5.61-25.8), positive attitudes (AOR = 5.68; 95% CI: 2.63-12.5), and satisfactory practices (AOR = 6.49; 95% CI: 2.62-16.8). Non-border residents were more likely to report positive attitudes (AOR = 3.07; p < 0.001) and satisfactory practices (AOR = 3.84; p < 0.001). Urban (AOR = 2.28; p = 0.003) and semi-urban (AOR = 2.19; p = 0.014) participants showed more positive attitudes. Attitudes and practices showed the strongest correlation (ρ = 0.793, p < 0.001), indicating a strong association between attitudes and preventive practices. CONCLUSION: The findings suggest that gaps in awareness and training are associated with suboptimal practices, highlighting potential areas for targeted educational interventions. Strengthening community-level awareness and preparedness may support public health readiness for potential Mpox outbreaks in Bangladesh.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox has recently re-emerged as a significant global health concern, highlighting the importance of public awareness and preventive measures. This study aimed to assess the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) regarding Mpox and its epidemiology, prevention, and control measures among the general population of Bangladesh.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional survey was conducted from June 2024 to September 2024 among 1002 participants across 16 districts. The study focused on the general population of Bangladesh, using a multi-stage random sampling approach following STROBE guidelines.
Core methods
Data were collected through a structured questionnaire covering sociodemographics, knowledge, attitudes, and practices. Both univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were used to determine associations between sociodemographic factors and KAP outcomes.
Main results
Out of 1002 respondents, only 37.2% demonstrated good knowledge of Mpox, while 74.0% had positive attitudes, and 80.0% reported satisfactory preventive practices. Higher education was strongly associated with good knowledge (AOR = 11.8; 95% CI: 5.61-25.8), positive attitudes (AOR = 5.68; 95% CI: 2.63-12.5), and satisfactory practices (AOR = 6.49; 95% CI: 2.62-16.8).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that gaps in awareness and training are associated with suboptimal practices, highlighting potential areas for targeted educational interventions. Attitudes and practices showed the strongest correlation (ρ = 0.793, p < 0.001), indicating a strong association between attitudes and preventive practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
Awareness of Mpox as a viral disease was limited (37.3%), and only 22.5% knew about the protective role of smallpox vaccination. Strengthening community-level awareness and preparedness may support public health readiness for potential Mpox outbreaks in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on self-reported data and associations rather than direct biological or clinical outcomes.

猴痘 传播 热点的地理空间分析和预测建模:以尼日利亚奥贡州为例

Geospatial analysis and predictive modelling of mpox transmission hotspots: a case study of Ogun State, Nigeria
作者： Adesina Simon Sodiya, Samuel Onuche-Ojo Egwu, Dada Olaniyi Aborisade, Saheed Akinbowale, Mary Idowu Olasoju
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是尼日利亚反复出现的公共卫生威胁,并于 2024 年 8 月被重新宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。尼日利亚的综合疾病 猴痘 和响应系统在地方政府地区 (LGA) 层面运行,但发布的 猴痘 风险模型仅在州层面解决风险,迫使异构地方政府地区进行统一部署。本研究开发并评估了奥贡州 20 个 LGA 的 LGA 分辨率地理空间风险层,并将其作为概念验证集成到实时 监测 平台中。40 例实验室确诊病例(NCDC,2022 年)和 151 例疑似病例(奥贡州卫生部,2022 年至 2024 年)的州级记录,总共 191 例,在空间上分解为 189 个伪存在点,并使用最大熵、随机森林和极端梯度提升的集合建模,超过 100 个引导重复。在随机保留下(AUC 0.77;博伊斯指数 0.99),歧视性良好,但在空间阻塞交叉验证(AUC 0.57)下,歧视性下降,表明该层适合于层之间的粗略分类,而不是层内排名。沿阿贝奥库塔南和伊杰布-雷莫轴线的中央走廊,有 7 个地方政府行政区被列为高风险,6 个为中风险,7 个为低风险。分层层导出为具有特定于层的操作包的 GeoJSON,并集成到 IDS-Master 平台中,作为从空间 流行病学 到常规公共卫生 流行病学 的概念验证途径。由于 189 个点是从总计数中分解出来的,而不是单独进行地理定位的,因此这些层应被解读为分诊的空间先验,而不是已确认的病例位置。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决尼日利亚反复出现的 猴痘 公共卫生威胁,特别是地方政府地区 (LGA) 层面需要更精确的风险模型,以改进有针对性的干预措施。
设计与对象
该研究使用来自 40 个实验室确诊病例和 151 个疑似 猴痘 病例的 191 个空间分解的伪存在点,评估了尼日利亚奥贡州的 LGA 分辨率地理空间风险层,并将其集成到实时 监测 平台中。
核心方法
研究人员使用了最大熵、随机森林和极端梯度增强模型的集合,并进行了超过 100 次引导复制来开发和验证地理空间风险层。
主要结果
地理空间风险层在随机保留(AUC 0.77;博伊斯指数 0.99)下表现出良好的区分度,但在空间阻塞交叉验证(AUC 0.57)下表现不佳,表明其适合粗略分类而不是详细排名。
解释与创新
该研究的分层风险层已集成到 IDS-Master 平台中,作为将空间 流行病学 转化为常规公共卫生 监测 的概念验证,为 猴痘 风险评估提供了一种新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
分层风险层可以通过为 猴痘 风险提供空间优先级划分工具,为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,改善资源分配,并加强尼日利亚奥贡州的 监测 工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究对伪存在点的依赖以及空间阻塞交叉验证下的较差性能限制了该层的精度和普遍性,这表明需要进一步验证。

Geospatial analysis and predictive modelling of mpox transmission hotspots: a case study of Ogun State, Nigeria

Authors: Adesina Simon Sodiya, Samuel Onuche-Ojo Egwu, Dada Olaniyi Aborisade, Saheed Akinbowale, Mary Idowu Olasoju
Original abstract (English)
Mpox is a recurring public health threat in Nigeria and was re-declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in August 2024. Nigeria's Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response system operates at the Local Government Area (LGA) level, yet published mpox risk models resolve risk only at state level, forcing uniform deployment across heterogeneous LGAs. This study developed and evaluated an LGA-resolution geospatial risk layer for the 20 LGAs of Ogun State and integrated it, as a proof of concept, into a live surveillance platform. State-level records of 40 laboratory-confirmed cases (NCDC, 2022) and 151 suspected cases (Ogun State Ministry of Health, 2022–2024), 191 in total, were spatially disaggregated into 189 pseudo-presence points and modelled with an ensemble of maximum entropy, random forest, and extreme gradient boosting over 100 bootstrap replicates. Discrimination was good under random hold-out (AUC 0.77; Boyce index 0.99) but fell under spatially blocked cross-validation (AUC 0.57), indicating that the layer is suited to coarse triage between tiers rather than within-tier ranking. Seven LGAs were classified high risk along a central corridor anchored on Abeokuta South and the Ijebu–Remo axis, six medium, and seven low. The tiered layer was exported as GeoJSON with tier-specific action packages and integrated into the IDS-Master platform as a proof-of-concept pathway from spatial epidemiology to routine public health surveillance. Because the 189 points were disaggregated from aggregate counts rather than individually geolocated, the tiers should be read as a spatial prior for triage rather than as confirmed case locations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the recurring public health threat of mpox in Nigeria, particularly the need for more precise risk models at the Local Government Area (LGA) level to improve targeted interventions.
Design and population
The study evaluated an LGA-resolution geospatial risk layer for Ogun State, Nigeria, using 191 spatially disaggregated pseudo-presence points from 40 laboratory-confirmed and 151 suspected mpox cases, and integrated it into a live surveillance platform.
Core methods
The researchers used an ensemble of maximum entropy, random forest, and extreme gradient boosting models over 100 bootstrap replicates to develop and validate the geospatial risk layer.
Main results
The geospatial risk layer showed good discrimination under random hold-out (AUC 0.77; Boyce index 0.99) but poor performance under spatially blocked cross-validation (AUC 0.57), indicating its suitability for coarse triage rather than detailed ranking.
Interpretation and novelty
The study's tiered risk layer was integrated into the IDS-Master platform as a proof-of-concept for translating spatial epidemiology into routine public health surveillance, offering a novel approach to mpox risk assessment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The tiered risk layer can inform targeted public health interventions, improve resource allocation, and enhance surveillance efforts in Ogun State, Nigeria, by providing a spatial prioritization tool for mpox risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on pseudo-presence points and the poor performance under spatially blocked cross-validation limit the layer's precision and generalizability, suggesting that further validation is needed.

Journal: Journal of Big Data  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-13  |  DOI: 10.1186/s40537-026-01482-w  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

AIRIME:一种可扩展的自适应 RIME 算法,用于特征优化和数据驱动的猴痘疾病预测

AIRIME: a scalable adaptive RIME algorithm for feature optimization and data-driven monkeypox disease prediction
作者： Mohamed Elhosseny, Mahmoud Abdel-salam, Doaa Sami Khafaga, Eman Abdullah Aldakheel, Rui Zhong, Ibrahim M. El-Hasnony, Ibrahim M. El‐Hasnony
摘要中文翻译最近应用中大数据的快速增长迫切需要能够处理大规模和高维问题的可扩展且高效的优化算法。为了解决这一挑战,提出了一种自适应改进雾凇-冰优化算法(AIRIME),该算法建立在基于物理的 RIME 算法的基础上,该算法受到表面软雾和硬雾凇形成的启发。虽然 RIME 提供了坚实的理论基础,但它受到有限的探索能力、缓慢的收敛以及探索与开发之间的不平衡的阻碍。AIRIME引入了四项关键增强功能,包括自适应k平均最佳突变(AKAB)、自适应搜索过程(ASP)、增强解质量(ESQ)和基于高斯的最优解突变(GBOM),以克服这些挑战并满足大数据应用的要求。AIRIME 在 CEC2017 和 CEC2022 测试套件的 41 项功能上进行了严格测试,性能优于各种经典、最新和高级算法。统计评估,包括 Friedman 等级和 Wilcoxon 等级检验,验证了其卓越的性能和稳健性。敏感性分析进一步证明了所提出策略的适应性和效率。此外,AIRIME 的二进制版本应用于 14 个数据集的特征选择 (FS) 任务,与各种已建立的二进制元启发法相比,取得了有希望的结果。此外,其在现实世界中的适用性在新型猴痘疾病预测框架中得到了证明,其中 AIRIME 帮助实现了 95.52% 的出色分类准确率,同时选择的特征明显减少,强调了其对高维医疗数据进行分类的效率。因此,AIRIME 代表了大数据驱动优化方面的重大进步,它将可扩展性、适应性和鲁棒性结合起来,以解决包括关键医疗保健应用在内的各个领域的复杂和高维挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决对可扩展且高效的优化算法的需求,以处理大规模和高维问题,特别是在大数据应用的背景下。这一背景凸显了此类算法在管理复杂数据集方面日益重要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在 CEC2017 和 CEC2022 测试套件中的 41 个基准函数上开发和测试 AIRIME 算法,以及将其应用于 14 个数据集以执行特征选择任务。该算法还评估了其在猴痘疾病预测框架中的实际适用性。
核心方法
AIRIME 算法通过四个关键策略进行了增强:自适应 k 平均最佳 突变 (AKAB)、自适应搜索过程 (ASP)、增强解质量 (ESQ) 和基于高斯的最优解 突变 (GBOM)。实施这些措施是为了提高算法处理高维数据的性能,并通过弗里德曼等级和威尔科克森等级测试等统计测试来验证其有效性。
主要结果
AIRIME 在 CEC2017 和 CEC2022 测试套件上的表现优于各种经典、最新和高级算法。敏感性分析证实了其适应性和效率,其二进制版本在特征选择任务中取得了可喜的结果。此外,它在猴痘疾病预测框架中表现出 95.52% 的出色分类准确率,同时选择的特征明显更少。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为 AIRIME 在大数据驱动优化方面取得重大进步的证据,特别是其解决医疗保健应用中复杂和高维挑战的能力。新颖之处在于它的适应性、可扩展性和现实世界使用的稳健性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,AIRIME 可通过优化高维医疗数据中的特征选择来提高疾病预测框架的准确性和效率,特别是猴痘。这对更好的 监测 和早期检测系统具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于基准函数和数据集,可能无法完全代表现实世界的复杂性。然而,这些结果得到了严格的统计评估的支持,表明对该算法的性能有一定的信心。

AIRIME: a scalable adaptive RIME algorithm for feature optimization and data-driven monkeypox disease prediction

Authors: Mohamed Elhosseny, Mahmoud Abdel-salam, Doaa Sami Khafaga, Eman Abdullah Aldakheel, Rui Zhong, Ibrahim M. El-Hasnony, Ibrahim M. El‐Hasnony
Original abstract (English)
The rapid growth of big data in recent applications has created a pressing need for scalable and efficient optimization algorithms capable of handling large-scale and high-dimensional problems. Addressing this challenge, an Adaptive Improved Rime-Ice Optimization algorithm (AIRIME) is proposed which is built on the physics-based RIME algorithm inspired by the formation of soft and hard rime on surfaces. While RIME offers a strong theoretical foundation, it is hindered by limited exploratory capability, slow convergence, and an imbalance between exploration and exploitation. AIRIME introduces four key enhancements, including Adaptive k-Average-Best Mutation (AKAB), Adaptive Searching Process (ASP), Enhanced Solution Quality (ESQ), and Gaussian-Based Optimal Solution Mutation (GBOM), to overcome these challenges and meet the requirements of big data applications. AIRIME was rigorously tested on 41 functions from the CEC2017 and CEC2022 test suites, outperforming various classical, recent and advanced algorithms. Statistical evaluations, including Friedman rank and Wilcoxon rank tests, validated its superior performance and robustness. Sensitivity analyses further demonstrated the adaptability and efficiency of the proposed strategies. Moreover, a binary version of AIRIME was applied to feature selection (FS) tasks across 14 datasets, achieving promising results compared to various established binary metaheuristics. Furthermore, its real-world applicability was demonstrated in a novel Monkeypox disease prediction framework, where AIRIME helped to achieve an exceptional classification accuracy of 95.52% while selecting significantly fewer features, underscoring its efficiency in classifying high-dimensional healthcare data. Thus, AIRIME represents a significant advancement in big data-driven optimization, combining scalability, adaptability, and robustness to address complex and high-dimensional challenges across various domains, including critical healthcare applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for scalable and efficient optimization algorithms to handle large-scale and high-dimensional problems, particularly in the context of big data applications. The background highlights the growing importance of such algorithms in managing complex data sets.
Design and population
The study involved the development and testing of the AIRIME algorithm on 41 benchmark functions from the CEC2017 and CEC2022 test suites, as well as its application to 14 datasets for feature selection tasks. The algorithm was also evaluated for its real-world applicability in a monkeypox disease prediction framework.
Core methods
The AIRIME algorithm was enhanced with four key strategies: Adaptive k-Average-Best Mutation (AKAB), Adaptive Searching Process (ASP), Enhanced Solution Quality (ESQ), and Gaussian-Based Optimal Solution Mutation (GBOM). These were implemented to improve the algorithm's performance in handling high-dimensional data and to validate its effectiveness through statistical tests such as Friedman rank and Wilcoxon rank tests.
Main results
AIRIME outperformed various classical, recent, and advanced algorithms on the CEC2017 and CEC2022 test suites. Sensitivity analyses confirmed its adaptability and efficiency, and its binary version achieved promising results in feature selection tasks. Additionally, it demonstrated exceptional classification accuracy of 95.52% in a monkeypox disease prediction framework while selecting significantly fewer features.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as evidence of AIRIME's significant advancement in big data-driven optimization, particularly in its ability to address complex and high-dimensional challenges in healthcare applications. The novelty lies in its adaptability, scalability, and robustness for real-world use.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that AIRIME can be used to improve the accuracy and efficiency of disease prediction frameworks, particularly for monkeypox, by optimizing feature selection in high-dimensional healthcare data. This has implications for better surveillance and early detection systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to benchmark functions and datasets, which may not fully represent real-world complexities. However, the results are supported by rigorous statistical evaluations, indicating a moderate level of confidence in the algorithm's performance.

北卡罗来纳州,美国,2023年10月–2025年5月污水中发现的猴痘病毒监测

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025
作者： Mara B. Tate, Virginia T. Guidry, Rachel T. Noble, Erica F. Wilson, Emma Doran, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Thomas Clerkin, Justin Albertson, Nicole L. Snyder, Ty Lautenschlager
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2023 年 9 月,北卡罗来纳州卫生与公众服务部在上次报告 5 个月后发现了 猴痘 分支 II 的新病例,强调需要增强 监测 来检测可能未被识别的 传播。
设计与对象
To assess possible unrecognized transmission, nonvariola orthopoxvirus testing was implemented at select wastewater sites during October 29, 2023–May 2025, across 16 treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents.
核心方法
During October 29, 2023–May 2025, a total of 801 samples were collected across 16 treatment plants serving ≈2 million residents. We analyzed wastewater detections (n = 47) and clinical cases (n = 45) using a +17-day window.
主要结果
识别下水道内存在 > 1 个时间对齐的 猴痘 病例的总体敏感性为 10.6%(95% CI 6.5%–16.7%);特异性为 95.1% (95% CI 93.2%–96.5%),阳性预测值为 31.9% (95% CI 20.4%–46.2%),阴性预测值为 83.2% (95% CI 80.3%–85.7%)。
解释与创新
将检测限制在检测限之上会降低灵敏度,但会增加特异性,这表明基于阈值设置的检测准确性的权衡。
科研与公卫意义
废水 监测 显示出较低的敏感性,但为公共卫生应对提供了补充意识,支持使用该方法作为临床监测的辅助手段。
局限与证据强度
研究发现废水监测的敏感性较低,这可能会限制其检测所有病例的有效性,但由于统计措施报告清晰,证据强度中等。

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025

Authors: Mara B. Tate, Virginia T. Guidry, Rachel T. Noble, Erica F. Wilson, Emma Doran, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Thomas Clerkin, Justin Albertson, Nicole L. Snyder, Ty Lautenschlager
Original abstract (English)
In September 2023, the North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services identified a new case of mpox clade II 5 months after the last previous report. To assess possible unrecognized transmission, nonvariola orthopoxvirus testing was implemented at select wastewater sites. During October 29, 2023–May 2025, a total of 801 samples were collected across 16 treatment plants serving ≈2 million residents. We analyzed wastewater detections (n = 47) and clinical cases (n = 45) using a +17-day window. Overall sensitivity of identifying the presence of >1 temporally aligned mpox case within a sewershed was 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%–16.7%); specificity was 95.1% (95% CI 93.2%–96.5%), positive predictive value was 31.9% (95% CI 20.4%–46.2%), and negative predictive value was 83.2% (95% CI 80.3%–85.7%). Restricting detections above the limit of detection reduced sensitivity but increased specificity. Overall, wastewater surveillance showed low sensitivity but provided complementary awareness for public health response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In September 2023, the North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services identified a new case of mpox clade II 5 months after the last previous report, highlighting the need for enhanced surveillance to detect possible unrecognized transmission.
Design and population
To assess possible unrecognized transmission, nonvariola orthopoxvirus testing was implemented at select wastewater sites during October 29, 2023–May 2025, across 16 treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents.
Core methods
During October 29, 2023–May 2025, a total of 801 samples were collected across 16 treatment plants serving ≈2 million residents. We analyzed wastewater detections (n = 47) and clinical cases (n = 45) using a +17-day window.
Main results
Overall sensitivity of identifying the presence of >1 temporally aligned mpox case within a sewershed was 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%–16.7%); specificity was 95.1% (95% CI 93.2%–96.5%), positive predictive value was 31.9% (95% CI 20.4%–46.2%), and negative predictive value was 83.2% (95% CI 80.3%–85.7%).
Interpretation and novelty
Restricting detections above the limit of detection reduced sensitivity but increased specificity, indicating a trade-off in detection accuracy based on threshold settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
Wastewater surveillance showed low sensitivity but provided complementary awareness for public health response, supporting the use of this method as an adjunct to clinical monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study found that wastewater surveillance had low sensitivity, which may limit its effectiveness in detecting all cases, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the clear reporting of statistical measures.

皮肤科实践中的 猴痘(以前称为猴痘):介绍转诊医院的一系列四个临床病例

Mpox (Formerly Known as Monkeypox) in Dermatological Practice: Presentation of a Series of Four Clinical Cases at a Referral Hospital
作者： Contreras Oceguera V, Ochoa Campollo M, Hernandez Gonzalez M, Hernandez Sanchez M, Castillo Gutiérrez J
摘要中文翻译然而,与最近的文献一致,我们的研究结果强调了显着的临床异质性:两名患者出现孤立的、疼痛的肛周病变,而一名患者在出现皮肤症状之前经历了最初的粘膜受累并伴有吞咽痛。重要的是,猴痘 的 诊断 并不是仅仅通过排除这些实体而建立的,而是通过 PCR 证实的,并在进行时得到了支持性的组织病理学结果。尽管 疫苗接种 不是本病例系列的重点,但对可用预防策略的认识补充了及时的临床识别和公共卫生遏制。我们的研究结果强调了对出现水疱脓疱、粘膜、生殖器或肛门生殖器病变的患者保持高度怀疑 猴痘 的重要性,特别是当存在流行病学危险因素时。发热、淋巴结肿大和不适等全身表现可能先于皮肤表现,但部分患者可能不存在,特别是患有局部疾病的患者[3]。到治疗的第18天,几乎所有病变都完全消退。随访4周时,没有观察到新的病变,所有皮肤和粘膜都已愈合,没有疤痕或重复感染。摘要 猴痘(以前称为猴痘)在全球范围内重新出现,成为一个重要的公共卫生问题,由于其主要的皮肤粘膜表现,皮肤科服务在早期识别中发挥着关键作用。我们介绍了 2025 年 4 月在墨西哥哈利斯科州一家三级皮肤病中心诊断出的一系列 4 例 猴痘 确诊病例。所有病例均发生在男男性行为者中,其中三人感染了艾滋病毒。临床表现多种多样,从播散性水疱脓疱...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在强调 猴痘 不断变化的临床谱,并强化皮肤科在早期识别和 诊断 中的作用。
设计与对象
该研究是对 2025 年 4 月至 2025 年 5 月期间在墨西哥哈利斯科州一家三级皮肤科中心诊断的四例 猴痘 确诊病例的回顾性病例系列。所有患者均为男男性行为者 (MSM),其中 3 例感染艾滋病毒,反映了在其他地区观察到的流行病学模式。
核心方法
诊断 通过 PCR 得到证实,并在执行时得到兼容的组织病理学支持。PCR 和活检结果与 猴痘 一致,并且彻底的性行为史和旅行史记录有助于诊断过程。
主要结果
所有患者均报告近期有过性接触,其中一名患者有超过 100 名男性性伴侣且近期存在高风险肛门接触。这些病例表现出临床异质性,包括孤立的肛周病变和粘膜受累并伴有吞咽痛先于皮肤表现。活检显示结果与 猴痘 一致。
解释与创新
作者解释说,猴痘 在皮肤病学实践中的临床表现多种多样,从经典的播散性疾病到局部肛门生殖器形式。这凸显了广泛区分 诊断 的必要性以及流行病学背景在早期检测中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了对水疱脓疱、粘膜、生殖器或肛门生殖器病变患者保持高度怀疑 猴痘 的重要性,特别是当存在流行病学危险因素时。他们还支持需要继续使用 监测 来更好地了解 传播 模式和临床行为。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于来自单个三级转诊中心的少量病例,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,确诊的诊断和一致的临床表现为观察到的模式提供了适度的证据。

Mpox (Formerly Known as Monkeypox) in Dermatological Practice: Presentation of a Series of Four Clinical Cases at a Referral Hospital

Authors: Contreras Oceguera V, Ochoa Campollo M, Hernandez Gonzalez M, Hernandez Sanchez M, Castillo Gutiérrez J
Original abstract (English)
However, consistent with recent literature, our findings highlight significant clinical heterogeneity: two patients presented with isolated, painful perianal lesions, whereas one experienced initial mucosal involvement with odynophagia preceding the development of cutaneous findings. Importantly, the diagnosis of mpox was not established solely through exclusion of these entities but was confirmed by PCR, with supportive histopathologic findings when performed. Although vaccination was not the focus of the present case series, awareness of available preventive strategies complements timely clinical recognition and public health containment.Our findings reinforce the importance of maintaining a high index of suspicion for mpox in patients presenting with vesiculopustular, mucosal, genital, or anogenital lesions, particularly when epidemiologic risk factors are present. AbstractMpox (formerly known as monkeypox) has re-emerged globally as a significant public health concern, with dermatology services occupying a key role in early recognition due to its predominant mucocutaneous manifestations. We present a series of four confirmed mpox cases diagnosed in April 2025 at a tertiary dermatology center in Jalisco, Mexico. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to highlight the evolving clinical spectrum of mpox and reinforce dermatology's role in early recognition and diagnosis.
Design and population
The study is a retrospective case series of four confirmed mpox cases diagnosed at a tertiary dermatology center in Jalisco, Mexico, between April 2025 and May 2025. All patients were men who have sex with men (MSM), with three living with HIV, reflecting the epidemiologic patterns observed in other regions.
Core methods
Diagnosis was confirmed by PCR and supported by compatible histopathology when performed. PCR and biopsy results were consistent with mpox, and the diagnostic process was aided by thorough sexual and travel history-taking.
Main results
All patients reported recent sexual exposure, with one patient having more than 100 male sexual partners and recent high-risk anal contact. The cases demonstrated clinical heterogeneity, including isolated perianal lesions and mucosal involvement with odynophagia preceding cutaneous findings. Biopsy showed findings consistent with mpox.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the clinical presentations of mpox in dermatologic practice are diverse, ranging from classic disseminated disease to localized anogenital forms. This highlights the need for broad differential diagnosis and the importance of epidemiologic context in early detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of maintaining a high index of suspicion for mpox in patients with vesiculopustular, mucosal, genital, or anogenital lesions, particularly when epidemiologic risk factors are present. They also support the need for continued surveillance to better understand transmission patterns and clinical behavior.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a small number of cases from a single tertiary referral center, which may affect generalizability. However, the confirmed diagnoses and consistent clinical presentations provide moderate evidence for the observed patterns.

HECA-YOLOv11:一种高精度混合注意力网络,用于实时猴痘皮肤病变检测并具有可解释性

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability
作者： Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
摘要中文翻译简介:全球 暴发 猴痘风险需要高效的诊断工具。目前的方法速度缓慢,并且难以区分相似的皮肤病变,而深度学习模型缺乏多类分类的准确性和可解释性。本研究旨在开发一种高性能、实时筛查工具,用于早期检测猴痘和类似病症。方法:本研究提出了一种增强的架构,将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制纳入 YOLOv11 中,以改进特征选择。HECA 对输入特征图执行全局平均池化 (GAP) 和最大平均池化 (MAP)。该研究利用空间变换和像素域增强,建立并增强了包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的四类数据集。梯度加权类激活映射 (Grad-CAM) 生成用于病变定位可视化的热图,而基于 PyQt5 的交互式诊断界面则简化了临床工作流程。结果:实验结果表明,与测试集上的现有方法(EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201 和 YOLOv11s)相比,所提出的模型具有优越的性能,具有增强的检测速度、准确度(98.9%)、精确度(99%)、召回率(99.3%)和 F1 分数(99.1%)。在所有评估指标中,HECA-YOLOv11 的性能均优于 SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11 超过 5.1%。热图分析证实了该模型对病变区域的关注,与医学推理相一致。讨论:集成的注意力机制和可解释性技术有效地解决了自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制。高性能和低延迟标志着可靠临床决策支持的重大进步,对早期 暴发 遏制具有重要意义。结论:HECA-YOLOv11框架为早期猴痘筛查提供了高度准确和高效的工具。其标准化界面在加速公共卫生应对和改善新发传染病的全球控制方面具有巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生对快速、准确的诊断工具的迫切需求,以减轻 猴痘 的全球 暴发 风险,强调现有方法速度缓慢,而且深度学习模型缺乏多类皮肤病变分类的准确性和可解释性。
设计与对象
作者开发并评估了一个深度学习框架 (HECA-YOLOv11),旨在使用四类图像数据集实时筛查 猴痘 和类似的皮肤病变。
核心方法
该架构将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制集成到YOLOv11中,将全局平均池化和最大平均池化应用于输入特征图,并使用空间变换和像素域增强来增强包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的数据集。
主要结果
在测试集上,HECA-YOLOv11 实现了 98.9% 的准确率、99% 的精确率、99.3% 的召回率和 99.1% 的 F1 分数,优于 EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201、YOLOv11s、SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11所有指标均为 5.1%。
解释与创新
作者将集成注意力机制和可解释性技术解释为有效解决自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制,标志着早期 猴痘 筛查的可靠临床决策支持的重大进步。
科研与公卫意义
HECA-YOLOv11 的标准化界面和高性能表明有可能通过早期检测加速公共卫生反应并改善对新发传染病的全球控制。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告数据集规模、地理或人群范围、外部验证或前瞻性临床评估,限制了结论的普遍性,且证据强度为中等。

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability

Authors: Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: The global outbreak risk of monkeypox necessitates efficient diagnostic tools. Current methods are slow and struggle to differentiate similar skin lesions, while deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class classification. This study aims to develop a high-performance, real-time screening tool for early detection of monkeypox and similar conditions. Methods: This study proposes an enhanced architecture that incorporates the Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11 to improve feature selection. HECA performs Global Average Pooling (GAP) and Max Average Pooling (MAP) on input feature maps. Using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements, the research establishes and augments a four-class dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images. Gradient-Weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) generates heatmaps for lesion localization visualization, while a PyQt5-based interactive diagnostic interface streamlines clinical workflows. Results: Experimental results demonstrate the proposed model’s superior performance compared to existing approaches (EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, and YOLOv11s) on the test set, with enhanced detection speed, accuracy (98.9%), precision (99%), recall (99.3%), and F1-score (99.1%). HECA-YOLOv11 outperforms SE-YOLOv11 and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all evaluation metrics. Heatmap analysis confirms the model’s focus on lesion regions, aligning with medical reasoning. Discussion: The integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques effectively address key limitations in automated skin lesion detection. The high performance and low latency signify a major advancement towards reliable clinical decision support, with important implications for early outbreak containment. Conclusion: The HECA-YOLOv11 framework provides a highly accurate and efficient tool for early monkeypox screening. Its standardized interface holds significant potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for rapid, accurate diagnostic tools to mitigate the global outbreak risk of mpox, highlighting that existing methods are slow and deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class skin lesion classification.
Design and population
The authors developed and evaluated a deep learning framework (HECA-YOLOv11) designed for real-time screening of mpox and similar skin lesions using a four-class image dataset.
Core methods
The proposed architecture integrates a Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11, applying Global Average Pooling and Max Average Pooling to input feature maps, and augments a dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements.
Main results
On the test set, HECA-YOLOv11 achieved 98.9% accuracy, 99% precision, 99.3% recall, and 99.1% F1-score, outperforming EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, YOLOv11s, SE-YOLOv11, and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all metrics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques as effectively addressing key limitations in automated skin lesion detection, marking a major advancement toward reliable clinical decision support for early mpox screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The standardized interface and high performance of HECA-YOLOv11 suggest potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases through early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report dataset size, geographic or demographic scope, external validation, or prospective clinical evaluation, limiting generalisability and leaving the strength of evidence moderate.

猫科动物口腔微生物组的宏基因组描述

Metagenomic Description of the Feline Oral Microbiome
作者： Qiuyan Li, Jiayang Zuo, Longlong Cao, Quanhui Yan, Zhong Peng, Dengyuan Zhou, Yi W, Zhixin Lei, Yi Weixue
摘要中文翻译摘要 家猫口腔微生物群构成了猫科动物和公共卫生的重要微生态系统,但国家层面有关细菌和病毒的综合数据很少。目前的工作表明,猫科动物口腔微生物群本质上是一个独立的生态系统,首先受地理和气候的影响,而畜牧业则增加了更微妙的控制。OAPGC 已经建立——一份包含 969 种高质量病毒 基因组 中 443 种原核生物的国家名单,细菌组似乎主要被假单胞菌门、芽孢杆菌门或拟杆菌门占据,其中超过 34% 的物种为未培养谱系。该病毒组主要是基于Caudoviricetes的,并且几乎一半的病毒是未分类的,这表明在这方面具有很大的新颖性。从功能上讲,大多数温带噬菌体似乎在裂解和溶原循环之间频繁变化,这是与环境波动相匹配的细菌稳态的严格调节。特别令人感兴趣的是,猴痘病毒未能出现在 1,454 个测试样本中的任何一个中——猫现在不是 储存宿主 或 媒介。总而言之,这些结果揭示了巨大的地理差异、大量的微生物“暗物质”和重要的病毒控制机制。他们为伴侣动物口腔微生物组制定了全国范围的基线,从而帮助评估猫科动物 发病机制、预测人类的 跨物种溢出 并实施基于“One Health”的方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述猫科动物口腔微生物组的特征,特别关注病毒的存在和分布,以更好地了解其在猫科动物和公共卫生中的作用。这一背景凸显了在这方面缺乏国家层面的细菌和病毒综合数据。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对猫科动物口腔微生物组进行国家级分析,检查了 1,454 个测试样本,以确定不同地理区域的病毒组和细菌组的组成。
核心方法
研究人员进行了宏基因组测序来分析口腔微生物组,生成了 969 种高质量病毒 基因组 的综合列表,并鉴定了 443 种原核生物物种。他们根据病毒分类对病毒组进行分类,并评估已识别病毒的功能作用。
主要结果
病毒组主要由有尾病毒纲组成,几乎一半的病毒未分类。细菌组主要以假单胞菌门、芽孢杆菌门和拟杆菌门为主,其中超过 34% 的物种是未培养的谱系。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,猫科动物口腔微生物组是一个受地理和气候影响的独特生态系统,病毒种群具有显着的功能多样性。该研究揭示了大量微生物“暗物质”的存在,并强调了病毒活动对细菌稳态的动态调节。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解猫科动物口腔微生物组及其在疾病 跨物种溢出 中的潜在作用提供了基线,提供了对可能影响人类 跨物种溢出 的生态动态的见解,并为 One Health 策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的报告,证据基于 1,454 个测试样本的全国样本。由于缺乏详细的实验数据并且依赖于宏基因组分析而没有进一步验证,对这些发现的信心是中等的。

Metagenomic Description of the Feline Oral Microbiome

Authors: Qiuyan Li, Jiayang Zuo, Longlong Cao, Quanhui Yan, Zhong Peng, Dengyuan Zhou, Yi W, Zhixin Lei, Yi Weixue
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The domestic cat oral microbiome constitutes an important microecosystem for both feline and public health, but integrated data on bacteria and viruses at the national level are few. The present work shows that the feline oral microbiome is essentially a separate ecosystem guided above all by geography and climate, with husbandry adding more subtle control. An OAPGC has been built—a national list of 443 prokaryotic of the 969 high-quality viral genomes studied, the bacteriome seems to be largely occupied by Pseudomonadota, Bacillota, or Bacteroidota, with > 34%of species as uncultivated lineages. The virome is mostly Caudoviricetes -based, and almost half its viruses are unclassified, an indication of great novelty in this context. Functionally, the majority of temperate phages appear to change frequently between lytic and lysogenic cycles,a tight regulation of bacterial homeostasis matched to environmental fluctuations. Of particular interest is the fact that monkeypox virus failed to appear in any of the 1, 454 tested samples—cats are not now reservoirs or vectors. Taken together, these results reveal great geographic variation, massive microbial“dark matter,”and vital viral control mechanisms. They set out a nation-wide baseline for companion animal oral microbiomes and thus help to evaluate feline pathogenesis, anticipate spillover into humans, and implement“One Health”-based approaches.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the feline oral microbiome, particularly focusing on the presence and distribution of viruses, to better understand its role in feline and public health. The background highlights the lack of integrated data on bacteria and viruses at the national level in this context.
Design and population
The study involved a national-level analysis of the feline oral microbiome, examining 1,454 tested samples to identify the composition of the virome and bacteriome across different geographical regions.
Core methods
The researchers conducted metagenomic sequencing to analyze the oral microbiome, generating a comprehensive list of 969 high-quality viral genomes and identifying 443 prokaryotic species. They categorized the virome based on viral classifications and assessed the functional roles of the identified viruses.
Main results
The virome was predominantly composed of Caudoviricetes, with almost half of the viruses being unclassified. The bacteriome was largely dominated by Pseudomonadota, Bacillota, and Bacteroidota, with over 34% of species being uncultivated lineages.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the feline oral microbiome is a distinct ecosystem influenced by geography and climate, with significant functional diversity in viral populations. The study reveals the presence of a large amount of microbial 'dark matter' and highlights the dynamic regulation of bacterial homeostasis by viral activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a baseline for understanding feline oral microbiomes and their potential role in disease transmission, offering insights into the ecological dynamics that may influence spillover into humans and informing One Health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level reporting, and the evidence is based on a national sample of 1,454 tested samples. The confidence in these findings is moderate due to the lack of detailed experimental data and the reliance on metagenomic analysis without further validation.

使用衰减更新过程预测性网络中的传染病流行

Forecasting of infectious disease epidemics in sexual networks using attenuated renewal processes
作者： William D. Green, Christopher E. Overton, Thomas Ward, Ciara V. McCarthy, Sebastian Funk
摘要中文翻译预测新出现的传染病 暴发 对于男男性行为者 (MSM) 的性网络来说尤其具有挑战性,其中 传播 可以由异质和过度分散的接触结构、混合分类性以及一次性和经常性伙伴关系形成。利用综合 MSM 网络的随机流行病模拟和来自主要城市的经验 猴痘 案例数据,我们系统地评估了五类短期预测模型,从随机游走方法到半机械衰减更新过程。使用连续排名概率得分(CRPS)评估模型性能。在异构网络中的模拟流行病和实证流行病中,更新模型通过反向 S 形曲线衰减,以解释高度连接节点的早期耗尽,始终优于替代模型,实现更平衡和可靠的预测。我们的研究结果提出了一种在高度过度分散的网络中进行稳健预测的实用方法,其中将网络效应与其他因素区分开来仍然具有挑战性,并且通常需要额外的数据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决预测男男性行为者 (MSM) 性网络中传染病 暴发 的挑战,其中 传播 动态受到复杂的接触结构和混合模式的影响。
设计与对象
该研究使用综合 MSM 网络的随机流行病模拟和主要城市的经验 猴痘 案例数据评估了五类短期预测模型。
核心方法
使用连续排名概率得分 (CRPS) 评估模型性能,以比较不同方法的预测准确性。
主要结果
在模拟流行病和实证流行病中,通过反向 S 形曲线衰减的更新模型都优于替代模型,在异构网络中提供了更加平衡和可靠的预测。
解释与创新
研究结果提出了一种在高度过度分散的网络中进行稳健预测的实用方法,在这种网络中,如果没有额外的数据,通常很难将网络效应与其他因素区分开来。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为改善性网络中的 暴发 预测提供了一个有价值的工具,可以为 监测 的公共卫生策略以及 猴痘 和类似疾病的干预提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于仅抽象的证据,虽然模型表现出一致的性能,但它们对其他环境的普遍性可能需要进一步验证。

Forecasting of infectious disease epidemics in sexual networks using attenuated renewal processes

Authors: William D. Green, Christopher E. Overton, Thomas Ward, Ciara V. McCarthy, Sebastian Funk
Original abstract (English)
Forecasting emerging infectious disease outbreaks is particularly challenging for sexual networks of men who have sex with men (MSM), where transmission can be shaped by heterogeneous and overdispersed contact structures, mixing assortativity, and both one-time and recurrent partnerships. Using stochastic epidemic simulations on synthetic MSM networks and empirical mpox case data from major cities, we systematically evaluated five classes of short-term forecasting models, from random walk approaches to semi-mechanistic attenuated renewal processes. Model performance was assessed using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS). Across both simulated and empirical epidemics in heterogeneous networks, renewal models attenuated by a reverse-sigmoid curve, to account for early depletion of highly-connected nodes, consistently outperformed alternatives, achieving more balanced and reliable forecasts. Our findings suggest a practical approach for robust forecasting in highly overdispersed networks, where distinguishing network effects from other factors remains challenging and usually requires additional data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of forecasting infectious disease outbreaks in sexual networks of men who have sex with men (MSM), where transmission dynamics are influenced by complex contact structures and mixing patterns.
Design and population
The research evaluated five classes of short-term forecasting models using stochastic epidemic simulations on synthetic MSM networks and empirical mpox case data from major cities.
Core methods
Model performance was assessed using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) to compare forecasting accuracy across different approaches.
Main results
Renewal models attenuated by a reverse-sigmoid curve outperformed alternatives in both simulated and empirical epidemics, providing more balanced and reliable forecasts in heterogeneous networks.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a practical method for robust forecasting in highly overdispersed networks, where distinguishing network effects from other factors is typically difficult without additional data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results offer a valuable tool for improving outbreak prediction in sexual networks, which can inform public health strategies for surveillance and intervention in mpox and similar diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and while the models showed consistent performance, their generalizability to other contexts may require further validation.
