Ectromelia 病毒重新编程 HeLa 上皮细胞中的非典型 NF-κB 信号通路

Ectromelia Virus Reprograms the Noncanonical NF-κB Signaling Pathway in HeLa Epithelial Cells
作者： Justyna Struzik, Lidia Szulc‐Dąbrowska, Michał Koper, Małgorzata M. Gieryńska
摘要中文翻译摘要:非经典核因子 κB (NF-κB) 通路调节适应性 免疫应答 和 抗病毒药物 先天免疫。正痘病毒,包括人类病原体天花病毒和人畜共患病毒,如牛痘病毒和猴痘病毒,已经进化出多种策略来抑制 宿主 抗病毒药物 信号传导。我们之前使用体外免疫细胞模型的工作表明,小鼠特异性 正痘病毒 ectromelia 病毒 (ECTV) 抑制非经典 NF-κB 信号通路。在这里,我们研究了 ECTV 对人上皮 HeLa 细胞中非典型 NF-κB 信号传导的影响。肿瘤坏死因子超家族配体用于激活或调节非经典 NF-κB 信号传导。使用荧光显微镜和免疫印迹分析,我们发现 ECTV 减少了 LIGHT (TNFSF14) 和淋巴毒素 α 1 β 2 (LTα 1 β 2 ) 诱导的 NF-κB 亚基 RelB 和 p52 的核积累。ECTV 单独感染增加了全细胞 RelB 和 NF-κB2/p100 水平,但没有显着改变核 RelB 水平。在 TNF-α 刺激的细胞中,ECTV 降低了细胞凋亡蛋白 2 (cIAP2) 细胞抑制剂的水平,而在 LTα 1 β 2 刺激的细胞中,ECTV 降低了 TNF 受体相关因子 3 (TRAF3) 的水平。RT-qPCR 分析显示 ECTV 上调 RELB 、 NFKB2 和 CHUK 表达。病毒感染还增加了 TNFAIP3 和 OTUD7B 的表达,这两种基因编码非经典 NF-κB 信号传导的调节因子。此外,受感染的细胞表现出选定的 RelB 响应基因的表达增加,包括 FBRS 、 CCR10 和 TNFRSF6B。总之,这些发现表明 ECTV 在体外调节与上皮细胞中非经典 NF-κB 信号传导相关的成分,但没有组成型通路激活的证据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在基于 正痘病毒 抑制 宿主 抗病毒药物 信号传导的先验知识,研究 ECTV 对人上皮 HeLa 细胞中非经典 NF-κB 信号传导的影响。
设计与对象
该研究使用人上皮 HeLa 细胞的体外模型来检查 ECTV 感染对非典型 NF-κB 信号传导的影响,重点是小鼠特异性 正痘病毒 ECTV。
核心方法
研究人员采用荧光显微镜和免疫印迹分析来评估 NF-κB 亚基的核积累,并使用 RT-qPCR 来测量与非经典 NF-κB 信号传导相关的基因表达水平。
主要结果
ECTV 减少 LIGHT 和 LTα 1 β 2 诱导的 RelB 和 p52 核积累,增加全细胞 RelB 和 NF-κB2/p100 水平,并上调 RELB、NFKB2 和 CHUK 表达。它还增加了 TNFAIP3 和 OTUD7B 的表达并选择了 RelB 响应基因。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,ECTV 调节上皮细胞中的非经典 NF-κB 信号传导成分,而无需激活组成型通路,这突显了一种新的免疫逃避机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解 猴痘病毒 如何操纵 宿主 免疫途径,这可以为未来关于病毒 发病机制 和潜在治疗靶点的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外模型,可能无法完全代表体内动力学,并且证据仅基于抽象数据,导致可信度评估适中。

Ectromelia Virus Reprograms the Noncanonical NF-κB Signaling Pathway in HeLa Epithelial Cells

Authors: Justyna Struzik, Lidia Szulc‐Dąbrowska, Michał Koper, Małgorzata M. Gieryńska
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The noncanonical nuclear factor κB (NF-κB) pathway regulates adaptive immune responses and antiviral innate immunity. Orthopoxviruses, including the human pathogen variola virus and zoonotic viruses such as vaccinia virus and monkeypox virus, have evolved multiple strategies to suppress host antiviral signaling. Our previous work using in vitro immune cell models demonstrated that the mouse-specific orthopoxvirus ectromelia virus (ECTV) inhibits the noncanonical NF-κB signaling pathway. Here, we investigated the impact of ECTV on noncanonical NF-κB signaling in human epithelial HeLa cells. Tumor necrosis factor superfamily ligands were used to activate or modulate noncanonical NF-κB signaling. Using fluorescence microscopy and immunoblot analyses, we found that ECTV reduced LIGHT (TNFSF14)- and lymphotoxin α 1 β 2 (LTα 1 β 2 )-induced nuclear accumulation of the NF-κB subunits RelB and p52. ECTV infection alone increased whole-cell RelB and NF-κB2/p100 levels but did not significantly alter nuclear RelB levels. In TNF-α-stimulated cells, ECTV reduced levels of cellular inhibitor of apoptosis protein 2 (cIAP2), whereas in LTα 1 β 2 -stimulated cells, ECTV reduced levels of TNF receptor-associated factor 3 (TRAF3). RT-qPCR analysis revealed that ECTV upregulated RELB , NFKB2 , and CHUK expression. Viral infection also increased expression of TNFAIP3 and OTUD7B , genes encoding regulators of noncanonical NF-κB signaling. Moreover, infected cells exhibited increased expression of selected RelB-responsive genes, including FBRS , CCR10 , and TNFRSF6B . Together, these findings indicate that ECTV modulates components associated with noncanonical NF-κB signaling in epithelial cells in vitro , without evidence of constitutive pathway activation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of ECTV on noncanonical NF-κB signaling in human epithelial HeLa cells, building on prior knowledge that orthopoxviruses suppress host antiviral signaling.
Design and population
The study used in vitro models of human epithelial HeLa cells to examine the effects of ECTV infection on noncanonical NF-κB signaling, with a focus on the mouse-specific orthopoxvirus ECTV.
Core methods
The researchers employed fluorescence microscopy and immunoblot analyses to assess nuclear accumulation of NF-κB subunits, and RT-qPCR to measure gene expression levels related to noncanonical NF-κB signaling.
Main results
ECTV reduced LIGHT- and LTα 1 β 2 -induced nuclear accumulation of RelB and p52, increased whole-cell RelB and NF-κB2/p100 levels, and upregulated RELB, NFKB2, and CHUK expression. It also increased TNFAIP3 and OTUD7B expression and selected RelB-responsive genes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that ECTV modulates noncanonical NF-κB signaling components in epithelial cells without constitutive pathway activation, highlighting a novel mechanism of immune evasion.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to understanding how mpox virus manipulates host immune pathways, which could inform future research on viral pathogenesis and potential therapeutic targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo dynamics, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

指动式微流控设备用于即时检测循环无细胞核酸提取

Finger-actuated microfluidic device for point-of-care circulating cell-free nucleic acid extraction.
作者： Ghulam Musaddaq, Labibah Ehsan, Yano Yoga, Joshua Castillo, Linda Marriott, Nicholas R Leslie, Deborah Williamson, Thomas Tran, Chuan Kok Lim, Maïwenn Kersaudy-Kerhoas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
及时准确的核酸提取是即时检测 (POC) 分子诊断的关键瓶颈,特别是对于血浆中低丰度的无细胞核酸(DNA、RNA、miRNA)。
设计与对象
我们推出了一种完全由手指驱动的微流体装置,用于无电源、无移液器的核酸提取。
核心方法
该原型设备集成了手指驱动阀、泵和泡罩试剂存储,实现了完整、直观的“样品到洗脱”工作流程,包括通过手动驱动在大约 35 分钟内进行裂解、结合、清洗和洗脱。
主要结果
在加标 DNA 的水样 (5-20 ng mL-1) 中,该设备的回收率为 84.6%,而实验室的回收率为 90.7%。该设备的临床应用是使用巨细胞病毒(CMV)、猴痘病毒(MPXV)或基孔肯雅热(CHIKV)阳性的患者样本进行试点。该设备成功实现了所有目标的 qPCR 扩增,病毒 DNA Cq 值仅需标准方案的 0.3 个循环。
解释与创新
该设备在 DNA 提取和各种目标的 qPCR 扩增方面的性能表明其在 POC 诊断方面的潜力,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
这种无需仪器的手动循环核酸提取工具可以帮助弥合基于侧流的核酸扩增技术与现实世界的分散诊断之间的差距,为资源有限环境中的传染病管理提供可扩展的解决方案。
局限与证据强度
Recovery from human pooled plasma samples were found to be on average 18, 62, and 552% higher than that obtained with the control bench extraction on initial sample volumes of 100, 75 and 50 μL, respectively. The device's performance was benchmarked against a commercial magnetic bead-based manual benchtop and an automated robotic workflow.

Finger-actuated microfluidic device for point-of-care circulating cell-free nucleic acid extraction.

Authors: Ghulam Musaddaq, Labibah Ehsan, Yano Yoga, Joshua Castillo, Linda Marriott, Nicholas R Leslie, Deborah Williamson, Thomas Tran, Chuan Kok Lim, Maïwenn Kersaudy-Kerhoas
Original abstract (English)
Timely and accurate nucleic acid extraction is a critical bottleneck in point-of-care (POC) molecular diagnostics, particularly for low-abundance cell-free nucleic acids (DNA, RNA, miRNA) in plasma. We present a fully finger-actuated microfluidic device for power-free, pipette-free, nucleic acid extraction. The prototype device integrates finger-actuated valves, pump and blister reagent storage, enabling a complete, intuitive, "sample-to-eluate" workflow, including lysis, binding, washing and elution via manual actuation in approximately 35 minutes. The device operation was benchmarked against a commercial magnetic bead-based manual benchtop and an automated robotic workflow. In DNA-spiked aqueous samples (5-20 ng mL-1), the device demonstrated recoveries of 84.6%, against 90.7% on the bench. Optimisation of magnetic bead volume identified 50 μL as the threshold for maximal recovery while minimising reagent consumption. Recovery from human pooled plasma samples were found to be on average 18, 62, and 552% higher than that obtained with the control bench extraction on initial sample volumes of 100, 75 and 50 μL, respectively. Coefficient of variation (CV%) for all techniques remained within reported laboratory ranges (20-80%). The device's clinical utility was piloted using patient samples positive for cytomegalovirus (CMV), monkeypox virus (MPXV) or chikungunya (CHIKV). The device achieved successful qPCR amplification for all targets, with viral DNA Cq values within as little as 0.3 cycles of the standard protocol. This instrument-free, manual circulating nucleic acid extraction tool could help bridge the gap between lateral flow based nucleic acid amplification technologies and real-world decentralised diagnostics, offering a scalable solution for infectious disease management in resource-limited settings.

兽医诊断技术在实验猴中的应用与展望

Application and prospects of veterinary diagnostic techniques in laboratory monkeys
作者： Zhang Z, Han D, Wei Q, Fu X, Yan Y, Li D, Xin J
摘要中文翻译本文综述了应用于实验室猴病监测的主流兽医诊断技术的现状和最新进展。我们分析了分子、免疫学、成像和传统微生物学方法在检测实验猴主要病毒、细菌和寄生虫病原体方面的具体应用、优势和局限性,涵盖定量 PCR (qPCR)、基于 CRISPR 的检测、ELISA、IFA/IHC 和其他已建立的平台。本次综述得出了几个具有直接实际意义的关键结论。首先,没有一种诊断技术是普遍适用的。诊断方法的选择应根据具体病原体(例如细菌、病毒或寄生虫)、样本类型(例如血液、BALF或组织)和临床情况(例如常规筛查、暴发调查或确证性诊断)来指导。第三,应进行成本效益分析,为实际部署决策提供信息,特别是对于资源有限的环境,先进技术可能过于昂贵。总之,实验室猴诊断的未来不在于用新兴技术取代现有方法,而在于它们的战略整合。实验猴作为与人类遗传关系最密切的非人灵长类动物模型,在生命科学研究、药物研发、生物制品评价等方面都不可或缺。然而,传染病要么在实验室猴群中流行,要么在繁殖和饲养过程中获得,例如 B 病毒和结核分枝杆菌,威胁动物福利和群落安全,同时也增加人畜共患 传播 风险并损害研究数据的可靠性。多样性、复杂性...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了实验室猴子对准确、高效的兽医诊断技术的需求,重点关注 患病率 和 B 病毒和结核分枝杆菌等人畜共患病原体的威胁。目的是评估各种诊断方法(包括分子、免疫学和成像技术)的优势和局限性,以便为它们在猴群管理中的战略整合提供信息。
设计与对象
研究设计涉及采用标准化方案的前瞻性验证研究,以评估实验室猴子中诊断平台的性能。该种群包括不同猴种和猴群中的多种病原体,例如 B 病毒和结核分枝杆菌。
核心方法
该研究采用了一系列诊断方法,包括 qPCR 和基于 CRISPR 的检测等分子技术、ELISA 和 IFA/IHC 等免疫学方法以及数字放射线照相和 CT 等成像技术。对这些方法在检测实验室猴子中的各种病原体方面的敏感性、特异性和适用性进行了评估。
主要结果
该研究报告了基于 qPCR 和 CRISPR 的检测分别在检测 B 病毒和猴痘病毒方面具有高灵敏度和特异性。B 病毒和 SIV 的同时感染在成年猕猴中很常见,感染率因地理和饲养条件而异。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了先进诊断技术在改善实验室猴子传染病的早期检测和精确识别方面的潜力。该研究强调需要进行成本效益分析和这些技术的战略整合,以解决猴群中复杂的流行病学现实。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果对于实验室猴传染病的早期检测、精确识别和有效治疗具有重要意义,为生物安全、生物安保和公共卫生做出贡献。兽医诊断与职业健康监测的整合确保了双向安全并与“同一个健康”框架保持一致。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认存在一些局限性,例如潜伏感染可能成为不受控制的混杂变量,以及需要进行成本效益分析。考虑到前瞻性验证研究以及需要进一步研究以确定新诊断平台在现实条件下的真实性能,证据强度中等。

Application and prospects of veterinary diagnostic techniques in laboratory monkeys

Authors: Zhang Z, Han D, Wei Q, Fu X, Yan Y, Li D, Xin J
Original abstract (English)
This review summarizes the current status and recent advances in mainstream veterinary diagnostic techniques applied to laboratory monkey disease surveillance. We analyze the specific applications, strengths, and limitations of molecular, immunological, imaging, and conventional microbiological methods for detecting major viral, bacterial, and parasitic pathogens of laboratory monkeys, covering quantitative PCR (qPCR), CRISPR-based assays, ELISA, IFA/IHC, and other established platforms. Several key takeaways emerge from this review with direct practical implications.First, no single diagnostic technique is universally applicable. The choice of diagnostic method should be guided by the specific pathogen (e.g., bacterial, viral, or parasitic), sample type (e.g., blood, BALF, or tissue), and clinical scenario (e.g., routine screening, outbreak investigation, or confirmatory diagnosis). Third, cost-effectiveness analyses should be conducted to inform practical deployment decisions, particularly for resource-limited settings where advanced technologies may be prohibitively expensive.In conclusion, the future of laboratory monkey diagnostics lies not in replacing established methods with emerging technologies, but in their strategic integration. As the non-human primate models with the closest genetic relationship to humans, laboratory monkeys are indispensable in life sciences research, drug development, and biological product evaluation. However, infectious diseases either endemic in laboratory monkey colonies or acquired during breeding and husbandry—for example, B virus and Mycobacterium tuberculosis-threaten animal welfare and colony security, while also elevating zoonotic transmission risks and compromising the reliability of research data. The diversity, complexity,...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for accurate and efficient veterinary diagnostic techniques in laboratory monkeys, focusing on the prevalence and threats of zoonotic pathogens like B virus and Mycobacterium tuberculosis. The objective is to evaluate the strengths and limitations of various diagnostic methods, including molecular, immunological, and imaging techniques, to inform their strategic integration in monkey colony management.
Design and population
The study design involves prospective validation studies with standardized protocols to assess the performance of diagnostic platforms in laboratory monkeys. The population includes a wide variety of pathogens, such as B virus and Mycobacterium tuberculosis, in different monkey species and colonies.
Core methods
The study employs a range of diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques like qPCR and CRISPR-based assays, immunological methods like ELISA and IFA/IHC, and imaging techniques like digital radiography and CT. These methods are evaluated for their sensitivity, specificity, and applicability in detecting various pathogens in laboratory monkeys.
Main results
The study reports high sensitivity and specificity for qPCR and CRISPR-based assays in detecting B virus and monkeypox virus, respectively. Concurrent infections with B virus and SIV are prevalent in adult macaques, with varying infection rates depending on geographic and housing conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the potential of advanced diagnostic techniques in improving the early detection and precise identification of infectious diseases in laboratory monkeys. The study highlights the need for cost-effectiveness analyses and the strategic integration of these technologies to address the complex epidemiological realities in monkey colonies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have significant implications for the early detection, precise identification, and effective surveillance of infectious diseases in laboratory monkeys, contributing to biosafety, biosecurity, and public health surveillance. The integration of veterinary diagnostics with occupational health monitoring ensures bidirectional safety and aligns with the One Health framework.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations such as the potential for latent infections to serve as uncontrolled confounding variables and the need for cost-effectiveness analyses. The evidence strength is moderate, given the prospective validation studies and the need for further research to establish the true performance of new diagnostic platforms under real-world conditions.

人工智能在传染病和临床微生物学中的应用

Artificial Intelligence in Infectious Diseases and Clinical Microbiology
作者： Agüloğlu Bali E, Çakır-Kıymaz Y, Akdemir İ, Alp E, Akçin Balan F, Büyükkörük M, Chaudhary R, Çınar G, Giacobbe D, Gülten E
摘要中文翻译本综述总结了当前机器学习(ML)、深度学习(DL)和大型语言模型(LLMs)在这些领域的应用情况,并对其优势和局限性进行了批判性评估。综述还讨论了人工智能(AI)在科学写作中的新兴作用以及其在临床应用中涉及的伦理和实际考虑因素。尽管已有大量研究报道了令人鼓舞的结果,但与外部验证、数据质量、模型泛化能力以及临床实施相关的重大挑战仍限制了这些技术的常规采用。本综述是基于2025年土耳其临床微生物学与传染病学会助理及青年专家委员会基础教育项目中进行的主题讨论、专家演讲和文献评估而撰写的,题为《人工智能在传染病和临床微生物学中的应用》。在这些教育活动的基础上,本综述对当前人工智能在IDCM(传染病和临床微生物学)中的应用证据进行了批判性综合,突出了其临床潜力以及在广泛实施前必须解决的方法学、实际和伦理挑战。人工智能(AI)正日益影响医疗保健领域,在传染病和临床微生物学中具有越来越重要的意义。尽管AI在图像分析、分子诊断、风险预测和临床决策支持方面表现出有希望的性能,但其常规实施仍受到外部验证不足、数据集异质性、算法偏差以及泛化能力和可解释性方面的挑战的限制。总体而言,现有证据表明,人工智能应被视为一种辅助决策技术,其安全性和有效性...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本综述旨在评估人工智能(AI)当前在传染病和临床微生物学中的应用,特别关注其改善诊断、抗菌管理和临床决策支持的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了现有文献和主题讨论,证据主要来自土耳其临床微生物学和传染病学会 2025 年基础教育计划的回顾性研究和专家演讲。
核心方法
该综述总结了机器学习(ML)、深度学习(DL)和大语言模型(LLM)在传染病和临床微生物学中的应用,包括它们在基因组流行病学、暴发监测中的应用,以及将多组学数据整合到预后模型中。
主要结果
基于人工智能的系统在受控环境中表现出较高的诊断性能,例如自动革兰氏染色解释和数字培养板分析,一致性超过 95%。然而,大多数人工智能系统仍然是特定于任务的,并且缺乏足够的前瞻性或多中心验证来进行常规临床使用。
解释与创新
作者解释说,虽然人工智能在提高诊断效率和支持临床决策方面显示出希望,但其在现实世界中的实施受到普遍性、可解释性和外部验证方面挑战的限制。新颖之处在于将多组学数据集成到基于机器学习的预后模型中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明人工智能可以提高诊断准确性并支持抗菌药物管理,但需要进一步研究来在不同的临床环境中验证这些工具并确保其在公共卫生应用中的有效性。
局限与证据强度
由于对回顾性研究的依赖以及缺乏跨不同医疗保健环境的前瞻性验证,证据受到限制。由于这些限制,这些发现的可信度较低。

Artificial Intelligence in Infectious Diseases and Clinical Microbiology

Authors: Agüloğlu Bali E, Çakır-Kıymaz Y, Akdemir İ, Alp E, Akçin Balan F, Büyükkörük M, Chaudhary R, Çınar G, Giacobbe D, Gülten E
Original abstract (English)
This review summarizes current applications of machine learning (ML), deep learning (DL), and large language models (LLMs) in these areas while critically evaluating their strengths and limitations. The review also discusses AI’s emerging role in scientific writing and the ethical and practical considerations associated with its clinical adoption. Although numerous studies have reported promising results, important challenges related to external validation, data quality, model generalizability, and clinical implementation continue to limit the routine adoption of these technologies.This review was prepared based on the thematic discussions, expert presentations, and literature evaluations conducted within the 2025 Basic Education Program of the Assistants and Young Specialists Commission of the Turkish Society of Clinical Microbiology and Infectious Diseases, entitled AI in Infectious Diseases and Clinical Microbiology. Building on these educational activities, this review critically synthesizes current evidence on AI applications in IDCM, highlighting both their clinical potential and the methodological, practical, and ethical challenges that must be addressed before widespread implementation. Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly influencing healthcare, with growing relevance in infectious diseases and clinical microbiology. Although AI has shown promising performance in image analysis, molecular diagnostics, risk prediction, and clinical decision support, its routine implementation remains constrained by limited external validation, heterogeneous datasets, algorithmic bias, and challenges in generalizability and explainability. Overall, current evidence suggests that AI should be regarded as a complementary decision-support technology whose safe and effective...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This review aimed to evaluate the current applications of artificial intelligence (AI) in infectious diseases and clinical microbiology, particularly focusing on its potential to improve diagnostics, antimicrobial stewardship, and clinical decision support.
Design and population
The study is a review of existing literature and thematic discussions, with evidence primarily derived from retrospective studies and expert presentations within the Turkish Society of Clinical Microbiology and Infectious Diseases' 2025 Basic Education Program.
Core methods
The review summarizes applications of machine learning (ML), deep learning (DL), and large language models (LLMs) in infectious diseases and clinical microbiology, including their use in genomic epidemiology, outbreak monitoring, and the integration of multi-omics data into prognostic models.
Main results
AI-based systems have demonstrated high diagnostic performance in controlled settings, such as automated Gram stain interpretation and digital culture plate analysis, with agreement rates exceeding 95%. However, most AI systems remain task-specific and lack sufficient prospective or multicenter validation for routine clinical use.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that while AI has shown promise in improving diagnostic efficiency and supporting clinical decision-making, its real-world implementation is limited by challenges in generalizability, explainability, and external validation. The novelty lies in the integration of multi-omics data into ML-based prognostic models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that AI can enhance diagnostic accuracy and support antimicrobial stewardship, but further research is needed to validate these tools in diverse clinical settings and ensure their effectiveness in public health applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the reliance on retrospective studies and the lack of prospective validation across different healthcare settings. The confidence in these findings is moderate due to these constraints.

治疗卵巢癌的聚(腺苷二磷酸核糖)聚合酶抑制剂 治疗 可改善盘状角膜炎,但停药后复发

Disciform Keratitis That Improves With Poly(Adenosine Diphosphate-Ribose) Polymerase Inhibitor Treatment for Ovarian Cancer but Recurs Upon Discontinuation of Treatment
作者： Fukushima A, Kohno S, Tabuchi H
摘要中文翻译这些发现表明,在因使用PARP抑制剂而免疫功能受损的癌症患者中,针对水痘-带状疱疹病毒(VZV)的免疫应答可能不会发生;然而,当停止使用PARP抑制剂并恢复免疫功能时,针对VZV的免疫应答会被重新激活,导致盘状角膜炎的复发。这些发现加强了对免疫应答在疱疹性角膜炎发病机制中的关键作用的理解[5]。有报道称,接受局部干扰素(IFN)-α2b治疗以治疗角膜表面鳞状细胞癌的患者可能会出现单纯疱疹病毒(HSV)相关性内皮炎[13]。关于系统性免疫状态影响病变消退或复发的断言缺乏实际免疫学数据的支持,因此该假设尚未被实证验证且存在局限性。总体而言,对于癌症患者而言,不仅在盘状角膜炎的诊断中,而且在确定任何眼科疾病的治疗策略时,都必须核实并充分了解其正在进行的医学治疗,并将这些信息与临床眼科发现相关联。该患者为一名73岁女性。她接受了卵巢癌手术,但由于在切除边缘观察到肿瘤细胞,需要进行化疗。她接受了尼拉帕利二水合物(一种聚(腺苷二磷酸核糖)聚合酶(PARP)抑制剂)治疗五年;然而,她出现了严重的乏力症状,影响了日常生活,使得继续治疗变得不可能。因此,停止了PARP抑制剂的使用,其身体状况得到改善。停药一年后,她出现了眼部带状疱疹,随后发展为盘状角膜炎...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨免疫状态与盘状角膜炎临床病程之间的关系,特别是在癌症 治疗 引起的全身免疫抑制的背景下。
设计与对象
本 病例报告 描述了一名有卵巢癌病史的患者,在停止 PARP 抑制剂治疗后出现盘状角膜炎,强调了该患者的临床病程和免疫状态变化。
核心方法
通过裂隙灯检查诊断盘状角膜炎,并通过房水 PCR 分析检测 VZV DNA。该患者接受口服伐昔洛韦和局部倍他米松治疗,观察到临床改善。
主要结果
PARP抑制剂治疗治疗期间盘状角膜炎得到缓解,该抑制剂诱导全身免疫抑制,但停药一年后复发。PCR 分析证实房水中存在 VZV DNA。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PARP 抑制剂的免疫抑制可能会阻止 VZV 重新激活,从而解决盘状角膜炎,而 治疗 停药后的免疫恢复可能会重新激活病毒,导致复发。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了监测癌症患者的免疫状态对于控制盘状角膜炎等眼部并发症的重要性,特别是与病毒再激活相关的并发症。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个案例,观察到的关联并不能确定因果关系。由于缺乏比较数据和免疫学验证,该证据被认为是中等的。

Disciform Keratitis That Improves With Poly(Adenosine Diphosphate-Ribose) Polymerase Inhibitor Treatment for Ovarian Cancer but Recurs Upon Discontinuation of Treatment

Authors: Fukushima A, Kohno S, Tabuchi H
Original abstract (English)
These findings suggest the possibility that in patients with cancer who are immunocompromised due to treatment with PARP inhibitors, an immune response against VZV does not occur; however, when immune function recovers upon discontinuation of the PARP inhibitor, the immune response against VZV is reactivated, resulting in the recurrence of disciform keratitis. These findings reinforce the understanding that the immune response is critical to the pathogenesis of herpetic keratitis [5].There have been reports of patients developing herpes simplex virus (HSV)-associated endotheliitis while receiving topical interferon (IFN)-α2b therapy for ocular surface squamous cell carcinoma [13]. The assertion that systemic immune status influences the resolution or recurrence of lesions lacks support from actual immunological data, and therefore, the hypothesis has not been empirically verified and has limitations.Taken altogether, for patients with cancer, it is essential, not only for disciform keratitis but also for determining treatment strategies for any ophthalmic condition, to verify and fully understand the details of their ongoing medical treatment and to correlate these with clinical ocular findings. The patient was a 73-year-old woman. She underwent surgery for ovarian cancer but needed chemotherapy because tumor cells were observed at the resection margin. She was administered niraparib tosilate hydrate, a poly(adenosine diphosphate-ribose) polymerase (PARP) inhibitor, for five years; however, she developed severe malaise that made daily life difficult, rendering continued treatment impossible. Consequently, the PARP inhibitor was discontinued, and her physical condition improved. One year after discontinuation, she developed ocular herpes zoster, followed by disciform ke...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the relationship between immune status and the clinical course of disciform keratitis, particularly in the context of systemic immunosuppression caused by cancer treatments.
Design and population
This case report describes a single patient with a history of ovarian cancer who developed disciform keratitis following discontinuation of PARP inhibitor therapy, highlighting the clinical course and immune status changes in this individual.
Core methods
Disciform keratitis was diagnosed via slit-lamp examination, and VZV DNA was detected using PCR analysis of aqueous humor. The patient was treated with oral valacyclovir and topical betamethasone, with clinical improvement observed.
Main results
Disciform keratitis resolved during PARP inhibitor treatment, which induced systemic immunosuppression, but recurred one year after discontinuation. PCR analysis confirmed the presence of VZV DNA in the aqueous humor.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that immune suppression from PARP inhibitors may prevent reactivation of VZV, leading to resolution of disciform keratitis, while immune recovery upon treatment discontinuation may reactivate the virus, causing recurrence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of monitoring immune status in cancer patients to manage ocular complications like disciform keratitis, particularly those associated with viral reactivation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case, and the association observed does not establish causality. The evidence is considered moderate due to the lack of comparative data and immunological verification.

关于儿童、儿童和青少年性暴力受害者的性传播感染的建议

Recomendaciones para el abordaje de infecciones de transmisión sexual en niños, niñas y adolescentes víctimas de violencia sexual
作者： Carolina Cerda Valenzuela, Natalia Talamona, Stephania Passalacqua Hidalgo, Daniel Cortés Guerra, Olga López Muñoz, Cecilia Piñera Morel
摘要中文翻译儿童、儿童和青少年的性暴力(NNA)构成了公共健康、暴力、精神和社会问题的严重问题。医疗并发症、性传播感染 (ITS) 代表了诊断、治疗和医疗法律、特别是儿科疾病的解决方案。在智利,存在成人暴力的问题,并且没有放弃 ITS 和 NNA 性暴力受害者 (VVS) 的完整规定。该文件由智利儿科协会 VIH 儿科委员会详细阐述,旨在为诊断、剖析、ITS 和 NNA 性暴力受害者的实际建议提供客观建议,并考虑最佳证据科学、现实的流行病学、规范和国家农业科学。方法论包括对国际和国内文学批评的修订、临床指导和专家共识。Se abordan las primes ITS relatedes en este contexto –淋病、衣原体病、滴虫病、sífilis、VIH/SIDA、甲型、B型和C型肝炎、单纯疱疹、人类乳头瘤病毒和猴痘–、detallando indicaciones de toma de muestras、períodos ventana、esquemas terapéuticos、profilaxis post展览、真空和血清学研究。建议智利 NNA VVS 采取预防感染措施,优化预防措施、机会和预防策略,并与国家规范保持一致。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
儿童、儿童和青少年的性暴力构成了公共健康、暴力、精神和社会问题的严重问题。这是一份关于性传播感染和大众感染的实际建议的文件。
设计与对象
该研究室对国际和国内文学批评进行了修订,以临床实践和专家共识为指导。在儿童、儿童和性暴力受害者青少年中,应考虑 ITS 的主要问题,考虑可靠的科学证据、流行病学的现实和国家规范。
核心方法
我们对 ITS 相关原理进行了分析,包括在上下文中详细说明了学生、期间、治疗、暴露后的轮廓、真空和血清学的相关指示。

解释与创新
建议对智利儿童、儿童和青少年性暴力受害者采取预防感染措施,优化预防措施、机会和预防策略,并与国家规范保持一致。
科研与公卫意义
建议在儿童、儿童和青少年性暴力受害人、儿童和青少年的临床实践中提供信息,并在智利背景下进行预防性治疗和实施预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该文件是对文学批评和专家共识的修订,限制了对问题的概括。证据是现代的,你没有报告原始研究或特定数据。

Recomendaciones para el abordaje de infecciones de transmisión sexual en niños, niñas y adolescentes víctimas de violencia sexual

Authors: Carolina Cerda Valenzuela, Natalia Talamona, Stephania Passalacqua Hidalgo, Daniel Cortés Guerra, Olga López Muñoz, Cecilia Piñera Morel
Original abstract (English)
La violencia sexual en niños, niñas y adolescentes (NNA) constituye un grave problema de salud pública, con consecuencias físicas, psicológicas y sociales a corto y largo plazo. Dentro de sus complicaciones médicas, las infecciones de transmisión sexual (ITS) representan un desafío diagnóstico, terapéutico y médico-legal, particularmente en la población pediátrica. En Chile, las guías existentes han sido desarrolladas para la población adulta y no abordan de forma integral el manejo de ITS en NNA víctimas de violencia sexual (VVS). Este documento, elaborado por el Comité de VIH Pediátrico de la Sociedad Chilena de Pediatría, tiene como objetivo establecer recomendaciones actualizadas para el diagnóstico, profilaxis, tratamiento y seguimiento de ITS en NNA víctimas de violencia sexual, considerando la mejor evidencia científica disponible, realidad epidemiológica, normativa y farmacológica nacional. La metodología incluyó revisión crítica de literatura internacional y nacional, guías clínicas vigentes y consenso de expertos. Se abordan las principales ITS relevantes en este contexto –gonorrea, clamidiasis, tricomoniasis, sífilis, VIH/SIDA, hepatitis A, B y C, herpes simple, virus del papiloma humano y Mpox–, detallando indicaciones de toma de muestras, períodos ventana, esquemas terapéuticos, profilaxis post exposición, vacunación y seguimiento serológico. Estas recomendaciones buscan estandarizar el abordaje infectológico de NNA VVS en Chile, optimizando la pesquisa precoz, el tratamiento oportuno y las estrategias preventivas, en concordancia con la normativa nacional vigente.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
La violencia sexual en niños, niñas y adolescentes constituye un grave problema de salud pública, con consecuencias físicas, psicológicas y sociales a corto y largo plazo. Este documento busca establecer recomendaciones actualizadas para el manejo de infecciones de transmisión sexual en este grupo poblacional.
Design and population
El estudio se basa en la revisión crítica de literatura internacional y nacional, guías clínicas vigentes y el consenso de expertos. Se aborda el manejo de ITS en niños, niñas y adolescentes víctimas de violencia sexual, considerando la mejor evidencia científica disponible, la realidad epidemiológica y la normativa nacional.
Core methods
Se analizaron las principales ITS relevantes en este contexto, detallando indicaciones de toma de muestras, períodos ventana, esquemas terapéuticos, profilaxis post exposición, vacunación y seguimiento serológico.
Main results
Se abordaron las principales ITS relevantes en este contexto –gonorrea, clamidiasis, tricomoniasis, sífilis, VIH/SIDA, hepatitis A, B y C, herpes simple, virus del papiloma humano y Mpox–, detallando indicaciones de toma de muestras, períodos ventana, esquemas terapéuticos, profilaxis post exposición, vacunación y seguimiento serológico.
Interpretation and novelty
Estas recomendaciones buscan estandarizar el abordaje infectológico de niños, niñas y adolescentes víctimas de violencia sexual en Chile, optimizando la pesquisa precoz, el tratamiento oportuno y las estrategias preventivas, en concordancia con la normativa nacional vigente.
Scientific and public-health significance
Las recomendaciones pueden informar la práctica clínica en el manejo de ITS en niños, niñas y adolescentes víctimas de violencia sexual, mejorando la detección temprana, el tratamiento adecuado y la implementación de estrategias preventivas en el contexto chileno.
Limitations and evidence strength
El documento se basa en una revisión crítica de literatura y consenso de expertos, lo cual puede limitar la generalización de los hallazgos. La evidencia es moderada, ya que no se reportan estudios originales o datos estadísticos específicos.

利用人工智能的真正潜力在复杂环境中进行卫生应急准备和响应

Harnessing the true potential of artificial intelligence for health emergency preparedness and response in complex settings
作者： Muhammad Tariq, Zubrain Aamer Ikram, Mona Elbarbary, Thomas Mollet
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索人工智能(AI)如何加强卫生应急准备和响应,特别是在东地中海地区(EMR)等复杂环境中,该地区卫生紧急情况频繁且资源紧张。
设计与对象
The study focused on the EMR, where as of 2025, WHO was responding to 16 concurrent health emergencies affecting over 115 million people. It examined the application of AI in health emergency contexts, including surveillance, outbreak detection, and response strategies.
核心方法
该研究讨论了使用机器学习、自然语言处理和大规模数据分析来处理健康相关信息。它强调了人工智能在加速 基因组 测序、支持 疫苗 开发以及实时监测疾病在多个国家传播方面的作用。
主要结果
研究发现,霍乱 暴发 的预测模型在资源有限的环境中准确率高达 99.62%。然而,它强调,这些模型需要对区域数据集进行外部验证,然后才能假设其准确性适用于电子病历或全球环境。
解释与创新
作者解释说,基于人工智能的风险模型可以帮助优先考虑预防性干预措施,并证明对准备工作的投资是合理的,从而可以显着节省成本并改善健康结果。这种方法的新颖之处在于其重点是将人工智能整合到公共卫生系统中以进行应急响应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,人工智能可以成为改善疾病 监测 和电子病历 (EMR) 响应的宝贵工具,因为电子病历中的突发卫生事件频繁且复杂。这可能会导致更有效的资源分配,并为未来的 暴发 做好更好的准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究指出,其他地方开发的预测模型需要对区域数据集进行外部验证,这限制了它们的直接适用性。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于现有的人工智能应用程序,并强调需要在特定于电子病历的环境中进行进一步测试。

Harnessing the true potential of artificial intelligence for health emergency preparedness and response in complex settings

Authors: Muhammad Tariq, Zubrain Aamer Ikram, Mona Elbarbary, Thomas Mollet

The Eastern Mediterranean Region (EMR) continues to bear a disproportionate share of the world’s health emergencies. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how artificial intelligence (AI) can enhance health emergency preparedness and response, particularly in complex settings like the Eastern Mediterranean Region (EMR), where health emergencies are frequent and resource constraints are significant.
Design and population
The study focused on the EMR, where as of 2025, WHO was responding to 16 concurrent health emergencies affecting over 115 million people. It examined the application of AI in health emergency contexts, including surveillance, outbreak detection, and response strategies.
Core methods
The study discussed the use of machine learning, natural language processing, and large-scale data analytics to process health-related information. It highlighted AI's role in accelerating genome sequencing, supporting vaccine development, and monitoring disease spread in real-time across multiple countries.
Main results
The study found that predictive models for cholera outbreaks achieved up to 99.62% accuracy in resource-constrained settings. However, it emphasized that these models require external validation on regional datasets before their accuracy can be assumed to apply to the EMR or global environments.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that AI-based risk modeling can help prioritize preventive interventions and justify investment in preparedness, which could lead to significant cost savings and improved health outcomes. This approach is novel in its focus on integrating AI into public health systems for emergency response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that AI can be a valuable tool for improving disease surveillance and response in the EMR, where health emergencies are frequent and complex. This could lead to more efficient allocation of resources and better preparedness for future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study notes that predictive models developed elsewhere require external validation on regional datasets, which limits their immediate applicability. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on existing AI applications and highlights the need for further testing in EMR-specific contexts.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

特考韦瑞 对人类 猴痘病毒 感染的 治疗 的功效:随机对照试验的系统回顾和荟萃分析。

Efficacy of Tecovirimat for the Treatment of Mpox Virus Infection in Humans: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials.
作者： Davi M Grecco, Luciano Araldi, Giordano C Soldera, João A B Zimmermann, João V Brisolara, Giovanna A Artero, Luiza F Streher, Maria E F Turatti, Harapan Harapan
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述评估了 特考韦瑞 对人类 猴痘病毒 感染的 治疗 的有效性和安全性,重点关注临床结果,例如皮肤病变临床消退的时间、第 28-29 天的病变消退以及 28 天全因 死亡率。
证据基础与方法
对随机对照试验 (RCT) 进行了系统回顾和荟萃分析,检索了从开始到 2026 年 6 月 5 日的 PubMed、Embase 和 Cochrane 对照试验中央注册库。使用 RoB 2 评估偏倚风险,并使用 GRADE 评估证据的确定性。使用具有限制最大似然估计和 Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman 调整的随机效应模型汇总危险比 (HR) 和风险比 (RR)。
共识与结论
Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups.
争议与缺口
未观察到统计异质性(两种结果的 I 2 = 0%)。然而,第 28 天的 死亡率 在这两项试验中都很罕见,排除了荟萃分析。该综述强调需要进一步的证据来确定选定的高危人群是否可以从 抗病毒药物 治疗中受益。
科研与实践启示
目前的随机证据不支持常规使用 特考韦瑞 来加速 猴痘 患者的临床恢复或提高 猴痘 率。需要进一步研究来探索其对特定高危人群的潜在益处。

Efficacy of Tecovirimat for the Treatment of Mpox Virus Infection in Humans: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials.

Authors: Davi M Grecco, Luciano Araldi, Giordano C Soldera, João A B Zimmermann, João V Brisolara, Giovanna A Artero, Luiza F Streher, Maria E F Turatti, Harapan Harapan
Original abstract (English)
Tecovirimat has been widely used for the treatment of mpox despite limited evidence supporting its efficacy. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials (RCTs) to evaluate the efficacy and safety of tecovirimat for this purpose. PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials were searched from inception to June 5, 2026 for RCTs comparing tecovirimat with placebo or standard care in patients with laboratory-confirmed mpox. Primary outcomes were time to clinical resolution of skin lesions, clinical resolution by day 28-29, and 28-day all-cause mortality. Hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects models with restricted maximum likelihood estimation and Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman adjustment. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2 and certainty of evidence using GRADE. Two low-risk-of-bias RCTs involving 941 participants were included. Tecovirimat did not shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase the lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). No statistical heterogeneity was observed (I 2 = 0% for both outcomes). Mortality at day 28 was rare in both trials, precluding meta-analysis. Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups. In conclusion, current randomized evidence does not support routine tecovirimat use to accelerate clinical recovery or improve mortality rates in patients with mpox, while further evidence is needed to determine whether selected high-risk populations may benefit from antiviral therapy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review evaluates the efficacy and safety of tecovirimat for the treatment of mpox virus infection in humans, focusing on clinical outcomes such as time to clinical resolution of skin lesions, lesion resolution by day 28-29, and 28-day all-cause mortality.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials (RCTs) were conducted, searching PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials from inception to June 5, 2026. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2, and certainty of evidence was evaluated using GRADE. Hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects models with restricted maximum likelihood estimation and Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman adjustment.
Consensus and conclusions
Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups.
Controversies and gaps
No statistical heterogeneity was observed (I 2 = 0% for both outcomes). However, mortality at day 28 was rare in both trials, precluding meta-analysis. The review highlights the need for further evidence to determine whether selected high-risk populations may benefit from antiviral therapy.
Research and practice implications
Current randomized evidence does not support routine tecovirimat use to accelerate clinical recovery or improve mortality rates in patients with mpox. Further research is needed to explore its potential benefits in specific high-risk populations.

猴痘 和性传播感染:猴痘 的并发流行病驱动因素和疾病结果 - 叙述性评论

Mpox and sexually transmitted infections: syndemic drivers of transmission and disease outcomes-A Narrative Review
作者： Ankita Ray, Tanu Bhati
摘要中文翻译2022 年全球爆发的 猴痘(原猴痘)暴发 成为突发公共卫生事件,对性传播感染 (STI) 患者构成更大威胁。该 暴发 的特点是 猴痘 和 STI 之间的重叠增加,反映了性网络中不断变化的 传播 动态。流行病学数据显示,人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)、沙眼衣原体、单纯疱疹病毒、梅毒等性传播感染经常与 猴痘 感染相关,这凸显了性接触在 猴痘 感染中的作用。使用 PubMed 和 Google Scholar 进行叙述性回顾,以确定 2008 年至 2026 年期间发表的相关文献,重点关注 猴痘、性传播感染、合并感染和妊娠结局。有证据表明,生物、行为和结构因素可能相互作用,影响 猴痘 传播 和临床结果。STI 合并感染可能与粘膜侵袭和免疫调节有关,可能影响疾病进展,但直接的机制证据仍然有限。尽管 猴痘 感染主要影响同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者 (GBMSM),但其在女性中尤其是怀孕期间的严重程度引起了对垂直 传播 以及孕产妇和新生儿并发症的担忧。本综述重点介绍了 猴痘-STI 合并感染的当前证据,并应用流行病框架来解释它们在生物、行为和结构背景下的共存。虽然这个观点提供了对 传播 模式的理解,但需要进一步研究来确定这些相互作用的程度。认识到这些因素对于筛查、早期 诊断 和减轻未来 暴发 和 猴痘 影响的管理策略非常重要。摘要:这篇关于 猴痘 和 STI 合并感染的叙述性综述探讨了流行病相互作用、猴痘 动态以及对疾病结果和公共卫生管理的影响

使用 PubMed 和 Google Scholar 进行叙述性回顾,以确定 2008 年至 2026 年期间发表的相关文献,重点关注 猴痘、性传播感染、合并感染和妊娠结局。
共识与结论
流行病学数据显示,人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)、沙眼衣原体、单纯疱疹病毒、梅毒等性传播感染经常与 猴痘 感染相关,这凸显了性接触在 猴痘 感染中的作用。
争议与缺口

认识到这些因素对于筛查、早期 诊断 和减轻未来 暴发 和 猴痘 影响的管理策略非常重要。

Mpox and sexually transmitted infections: syndemic drivers of transmission and disease outcomes-A Narrative Review

Authors: Ankita Ray, Tanu Bhati
Original abstract (English)
The worldwide outbreak of mpox (formerly monkeypox) in 2022 emerged as a public health emergency posing greater threat to persons with sexually transmitted infections (STIs). This outbreak featured an increase in overlap between mpox and STIs reflecting evolving transmission dynamics in sexual networks. Epidemiological data reveal that STIs such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), Chlamydia trachomatis , herpes simplex virus, syphilis have been frequently associated with mpox infection highlighting the role of sexual contact in its transmission. A narrative review was conducted using PubMed and Google Scholar to identify relevant literature published between 2008 and 2026, focusing on mpox, STIs, co-infections, and pregnancy outcomes. Evidence suggests that biological, behavioural and structural factors may interact to influence mpox transmission and clinical outcomes. STI co-infection may be associated with mucosal invasion and immune modulation plausibly affecting disease progression, however direct mechanistic evidence remains limited. Although mpox infection has majorly affected gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men (GBMSM), its severity in women particularly during pregnancy raises concerns regarding vertical transmission and maternal and neonatal complications. This review highlights current evidence on mpox-STI co-infections, and applies a syndemic framework to interpret their co-occurrence within biological, behavioural and structural contexts. While this perspective provides understanding regarding transmission patterns, further research is needed to establish the extent of these interactions. Recognizing these factors is important for screening, early diagnosis and management strategies to mitigate future outbreaks and impact of mpox. Summary: This narrative review on mpox and STI co-infections explores syndemic interactions, transmission dynamics and implications for disease outcomes and public health management
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The worldwide outbreak of mpox (formerly monkeypox) in 2022 emerged as a public health emergency posing greater threat to persons with sexually transmitted infections (STIs).
Evidence base and method
A narrative review was conducted using PubMed and Google Scholar to identify relevant literature published between 2008 and 2026, focusing on mpox, STIs, co-infections, and pregnancy outcomes.
Consensus and conclusions
Epidemiological data reveal that STIs such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), Chlamydia trachomatis, herpes simplex virus, syphilis have been frequently associated with mpox infection highlighting the role of sexual contact in its transmission.
Controversies and gaps
STI co-infection may be associated with mucosal invasion and immune modulation plausibly affecting disease progression, however direct mechanistic evidence remains limited.
Research and practice implications
Recognizing these factors is important for screening, early diagnosis and management strategies to mitigate future outbreaks and impact of mpox.

撒哈拉以南非洲地区医护人员应对猴痘大流行的准备情况:快速回顾调查知识差距

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap
作者： Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了改善卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 准备情况的迫切需要。尽管“同一个健康”方法具有潜力,但目前的实施仍然分散。本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。方法:按照系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。结果:纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。结论:尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。

范围与问题
猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 迫切需要加强做好准备。

遵循系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。
共识与结论
纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。

尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
科研与实践启示
本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap

Authors: Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
Original abstract (English)
Background: Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs). Despite the potential of a One Health approach, current implementations remain fragmented. This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications. Methods: Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical CrossSectional Studies. Results: Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels. Conclusion: Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs).
Evidence base and method
Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels.
Controversies and gaps
Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
Research and practice implications
This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications.

RNA和DNA病毒感染中细胞死亡信号传导的比较分析和潜在的抑制策略

A comparative analysis of cell death signalling in RNA and DNA virus infections and potential inhibitory strategies
作者： Panagiotis Κeramidas, Maria Pitou, Amalia Aggeli, Sotiris Varlamis, Theodora Choli‐Papadopoulou, Panagiotis Keramidas, Theodora Choli-Papadopoulou
摘要中文翻译最近的COVID-19大流行和猴痘的流行病暴发表明,病毒尽管结构看似简单,但却是复杂的微生物,已经进化出了复杂的促生存机制。它们操纵细胞死亡途径的潜力可以影响和决定感染周期和 宿主 的 免疫应答 的结果。RNA病毒通常表现出更急性和更具攻击性的性质,因为它们经常促进细胞死亡。通过这种方式,它们增殖并释放病毒粒子以感染邻近细胞并延续其生命周期。RNA 病毒感染的常见后果包括 宿主 中的过度炎症反应,通常导致上皮屏障受损。相反,DNA病毒调节这些途径以维持裂解周期(在裂解周期中它们进行复制)和潜伏期(它们在适当的刺激触发重新激活之前保持不活动状态)之间的平衡。实现这种平衡和感染阶段之间的过渡需要抑制细胞死亡过程。在这两种情况下,病毒似乎都会对细胞环境做出反应,通过 突变 增强其适应性,从而欺骗 宿主 防御,从而确保其生存。这凸显了理解这些生物威胁诱导细胞死亡的过程的必要性,从而促进广谱或靶向 抗病毒药物 治疗 的开发。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 RNA 和 DNA 病毒感染中的细胞死亡信号传导机制,特别强调 猴痘病毒。它探讨了这些病毒如何操纵 宿主 细胞死亡途径来影响感染周期和 免疫应答,以及这些知识如何为 抗病毒药物 治疗 的发展提供信息。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 RNA 和 DNA 病毒感染中细胞死亡信号传导的比较分析。它没有明确报告所使用的数据库、检索日期或资格标准。综述方法主要是叙述性的,一般观察和比较来自关于病毒感染及其与 宿主 细胞死亡途径相互作用的现有文献。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,RNA 病毒通常会促进细胞死亡,从而通过破坏上皮屏障并引发过度炎症反应来促进其复制和传播。另一方面,DNA 病毒调节细胞死亡途径,以维持其生命周期的裂解期和潜伏期之间的平衡,这对于它们的生存和重新激活至关重要。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,病毒操纵细胞死亡途径的机制尚未完全了解,特别是在特定病毒株及其与不同 宿主 背景的相互作用的背景下。对于 猴痘病毒 这些过程所涉及的精确分子途径也缺乏详细的研究。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进一步研究 猴痘病毒 操纵细胞死亡的分子机制。它还表明,了解这些机制可能有助于开发靶向 抗病毒药物 治疗 以及增强 宿主 免疫应答 抗病毒感染的策略。

A comparative analysis of cell death signalling in RNA and DNA virus infections and potential inhibitory strategies

Authors: Panagiotis Κeramidas, Maria Pitou, Amalia Aggeli, Sotiris Varlamis, Theodora Choli‐Papadopoulou, Panagiotis Keramidas, Theodora Choli-Papadopoulou
Original abstract (English)
The recent COVID-19 pandemic and the epidemic outbreak of Mpox have demonstrated that viruses, despite their apparently simple structure, are sophisticated microorganisms that have evolved complicated pro-survival mechanisms. Their potential to manipulate cell death pathways can affect and determine the outcome of the infection cycle and the host's immune response. RNA viruses typically display a more acute and aggressive nature, as they often promote cell death. In this way, they proliferate and release their virions to infect adjacent cells and perpetuate their life cycle. Common consequences of RNA virus infection include hyperinflammatory responses in hosts, typically leading in damage to the epithelial barrier. Conversely, DNA viruses modulate these pathways to sustain a balance between the lytic cycle, during which they replicate, and latency, wherein they remain inactive until an appropriate stimulus triggers reactivation. Achieving this balance and transitioning between stages of infection requires the inhibition of cellular death processes. In both instances, the viruses seem to respond to the cellular context, enhancing their adaptability through mutations, so to deceive host defenses and therefore secure their survival. This highlights the necessity to comprehend the processes via which these biological threats induce cell death, thereby facilitating the development of broad-spectrum or targeted antiviral treatments.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the mechanisms of cell death signalling in RNA and DNA virus infections, with a particular emphasis on the Mpox virus. It explores how these viruses manipulate host cell death pathways to influence the infection cycle and immune response, and how this knowledge can inform the development of antiviral treatments.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comparative analysis of cell death signalling in RNA and DNA virus infections. It does not explicitly report on the databases used, search dates, or eligibility criteria. The review method is primarily narrative, with general observations and comparisons drawn from existing literature on viral infections and their interactions with host cell death pathways.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that RNA viruses typically promote cell death, which facilitates their replication and spread by damaging the epithelial barrier and triggering hyperinflammatory responses. DNA viruses, on the other hand, modulate cell death pathways to maintain a balance between the lytic and latent phases of their life cycle, which is essential for their survival and reactivation.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that the mechanisms by which viruses manipulate cell death pathways are not fully understood, particularly in the context of specific viral strains and their interactions with different host contexts. There is also a lack of detailed studies on the precise molecular pathways involved in these processes for the Mpox virus.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further research into the molecular mechanisms of cell death manipulation by the Mpox virus. It also suggests that understanding these mechanisms could lead to the development of targeted antiviral treatments and strategies to enhance host immune responses against viral infections.

尼日利亚的气候变化、生态系统丧失和新出现的传染病

Climate Change, Ecosystem Loss, and Emerging Infectious Diseases in Nigeria
作者： Taiwo Bakare-Abidola, Aluna Elizabeth Ese, Victoria Abiodun, Elekwa Phillip, Olayemi Alice Oguntunde, Abdulsamad Afolarin Ahmed, Demilade Vincent, Victoria Ifeyinwa Uwakwe, Mayowa Olasupo, Goodness Promise Ibiebara
摘要中文翻译气候变化和生态系统退化会影响传染病危害、人类接触和对 暴发 的识别,但这些影响的方向和强度因病原体而异。尼日利亚提供了一个相关的环境,因为与 储存宿主 相关的感染、蚊媒疾病、水传播的 暴发 和气候敏感的呼吸道感染发生在截然不同的生态区和不平等的医疗环境中。这篇批判性叙述性评论评估了有关气候变化、土地利用变化、生物多样性、传播 生态和疾病 监测 的文献,优先考虑尼日利亚的实证工作,并利用区域或全球研究来阐明机制,而不是替代当地证据。尼日利亚与环境相关的最强分析涉及拉沙热,其地理 发病率 与气候、土地利用和社会经济因素相关,尽管回顾性预测并未建立前瞻性预测效用。啮齿动物实地研究表明,季节性 宿主 丰度和病毒检测不一定会同步变化。登革热调查发现了未被充分认识的发热性疾病和适宜的蚊子栖息地,但抗体、媒介 和分子结果具有不同的推论意义。黄热病模式与免疫和监测选择密不可分。对于霍乱,洪水和气候异常与供水系统故障、流离失所和卫生条件劣势相互作用;生态系统损失的直接归因仍然有限。脑膜炎和 猴痘 进一步说明了将一种生态机制扩展到根本不同的 传播 系统的危险。持续存在的局限性包括空间聚集、不完整的诊断分母、异质环境测量以及生态系统扰动、受感染的 宿主 和已确诊的人类感染的纵向联系稀缺。该综合报告主张针对病原体的 One Health 监测、前瞻性生态临床 队列研究、警告的样本外验证以及水的控制评估、媒介 和家庭干预措施。气候适应和生态系统保护提供了重要的机会,但传染病的好处应该被证明而不是假设。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了气候变化、生态系统丧失和新出现的传染病之间的关系,重点是 猴痘病毒。它探讨了环境变化如何影响 传播 动态、宿主 丰度和 猴痘病毒 的疾病识别,特别是在尼日利亚多样化的生态区和医疗保健系统的背景下。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对有关气候变化、土地利用变化、生物多样性、传播 生态学和疾病 监测 的文献的重要叙述性综合。它优先考虑尼日利亚的实证研究,并利用区域或全球研究来阐明机制,同时强调需要当地证据。该综述不报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是侧重于定性综合。
共识与结论
该综述强调,猴痘病毒 传播 受到生态和气候因素的影响,但证据有限且异质。它强调了病原体特异性 One Health 监测的重要性以及前瞻性生态临床 队列研究 的需要,以更好地了解环境变化与 猴痘 暴发 之间的相互作用。
争议与缺口
将生态系统损失与 猴痘 猴痘 联系起来的直接证据有限,并且审查指出,天花病毒或天花等相关病毒的发现不能直接应用于 猴痘。此外,该综述还发现了将生态系统扰乱、感染的 宿主 和已确诊的人类感染联系起来的纵向研究中的差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议对生态警报进行样本外验证,对水、媒介 和家庭干预措施进行控制评估,并开发前瞻性生态临床 队列研究,以提高对 猴痘 猴痘 猴痘 与环境变化相关动态的理解。

Climate Change, Ecosystem Loss, and Emerging Infectious Diseases in Nigeria

Authors: Taiwo Bakare-Abidola, Aluna Elizabeth Ese, Victoria Abiodun, Elekwa Phillip, Olayemi Alice Oguntunde, Abdulsamad Afolarin Ahmed, Demilade Vincent, Victoria Ifeyinwa Uwakwe, Mayowa Olasupo, Goodness Promise Ibiebara
Original abstract (English)
Climate change and ecosystem degradation can affect infectious-disease hazards, human exposure and the recognition of outbreaks, but the direction and strength of those influences differ among pathogens. Nigeria provides a pertinent setting because reservoir-associated infections, mosquito-borne diseases, waterborne outbreaks and climatically sensitive respiratory infections occur across contrasting ecological zones and unequal healthcare environments. This critical narrative review evaluates literature on climatic variability, land-use change, biodiversity, transmission ecology and disease surveillance, giving priority to Nigerian empirical work and using regional or global studies to clarify mechanisms rather than substitute for local evidence. The strongest environment-linked Nigerian analyses concern Lassa fever, whose geographical incidence correlates with climate, land use and socioeconomic factors, although retrospective prediction does not establish prospective forecast utility. Rodent field studies show that seasonal host abundance and viral detection do not necessarily move together. Dengue investigations identify under-recognised febrile illness and competent mosquito habitats, but antibody, vector and molecular results have different inferential meanings. Yellow fever patterns are inseparable from immunity and surveillance selection. For cholera, floods and climatic anomalies interact with water-system failures, displacement and sanitation disadvantage; direct attribution to ecosystem loss remains limited. Meningitis and mpox further illustrate the danger of extending one ecological mechanism across fundamentally different transmission systems. Persistent limitations include spatial aggregation, incomplete diagnostic denominators, heterogeneous environmental measurements and scarce longitudinal linkage of ecosystem disturbance, infected hosts and confirmed human infection. The synthesis argues for pathogen-specific One Health monitoring, prospective ecological–clinical cohorts, out-of-sample validation of warnings and controlled evaluation of water, vector and household interventions. Climate adaptation and ecosystem protection offer important opportunities, but infectious-disease benefits should be demonstrated rather than presumed.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the relationship between climate change, ecosystem loss, and emerging infectious diseases, with a focus on the Mpox virus. It explores how environmental changes influence the transmission dynamics, host abundance, and disease recognition of the Mpox virus, particularly in the context of Nigeria's diverse ecological zones and healthcare systems.
Evidence base and method
The review is a critical narrative synthesis of literature on climatic variability, land-use change, biodiversity, transmission ecology, and disease surveillance. It prioritizes Nigerian empirical studies and uses regional or global research to clarify mechanisms, while emphasizing the need for local evidence. The review does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but instead focuses on qualitative synthesis.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that Mpox virus transmission is influenced by ecological and climatic factors, but the evidence is limited and heterogeneous. It underscores the importance of pathogen-specific One Health monitoring and the need for prospective ecological–clinical cohorts to better understand the interplay between environmental changes and Mpox outbreaks.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited direct evidence linking ecosystem loss to Mpox outbreaks, and the review notes that findings from related viruses like variola virus or smallpox cannot be directly applied to Mpox. Additionally, the review identifies gaps in longitudinal studies that link ecosystem disturbance, infected hosts, and confirmed human infections.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends out-of-sample validation of ecological warnings, controlled evaluation of water, vector, and household interventions, and the development of prospective ecological–clinical cohorts to improve understanding of Mpox transmission dynamics in relation to environmental changes.

从性别特异性医学到异种皮质:关于医学和生物学当前前沿的三角研究简报 — 前沿进展研究简报,2026 年 10 月

From Sex-Specific Medicine to Xenocortication: A Triangulated Research Brief on Current Frontiers in Medicine and Biology — A Research Brief on Frontier Progress, October 2026
作者： Dandan Wang
摘要中文翻译这份研究简报将当前两期领先的国际期刊与预期的评论文献进行了三角测量。它首先总结了国际领先科学期刊(第 6819 卷)主题期刊中的七篇文章(三篇原创研究和四篇评论),该期刊致力于特定性别和女性健康,涵盖新陈代谢、癌症、骨关节炎、孕产妇生理学、性染色体、更年期和慢性疼痛。然后,它回顾了配套国际研究期刊(第 8135 卷)中的七项原创研究,涵盖人鼠异种皮质、促进睡眠的皮质回路、mTORC1 相关的长寿、肿瘤引流淋巴结检查点、染色体外 DNA 维持和猴痘病毒复制体。最后,它阅读了这 14 项前沿研究,针对 2023 年发表的 11 项综合研究——关于视网膜衰老中的蛋白质稳态、兴奋性毒性和神经修复、检查点细胞学、单细胞注释、眼部成像、比较药理学和 疫苗 犹豫——显示每一项研究如何预测当前前沿的主题。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究简介重点关注猴痘病毒(正痘病毒 猴痘)、MPXV 分支和 猴痘 疾病。它探讨了该病毒的生物学和分类学关系、其疾病表现以及与其机制和影响相关的当前研究前沿。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了来自国际领先科学期刊(Vol.)两期主题的 14 项前沿研究,包括 7 项原创研究和 7 篇综述,还引用了 2023 年发表的 11 项综合研究,涵盖视网膜衰老中的蛋白质稳态、兴奋性毒性、神经修复和 疫苗 犹豫等主题,以展示他们如何预测当前的研究主题。
共识与结论
该综述强调,猴痘病毒及其进化枝(I 和 II)对于了解 猴痘 疾病至关重要。它强调了研究病毒生物学(包括其复制体)及其在疾病进展中的作用的重要性。该评论还指出,这些研究预测了目前处于前沿的主题,例如病毒与 宿主 因素之间的相互作用。
争议与缺口
证据基础显示研究中存在一些异质性,特别是在解释病毒在疾病中的作用及其与 宿主 系统的相互作用方面。关于猴痘病毒复制的具体机制及其进化枝对疾病结果的长期影响,尚有待解决的问题。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,未来的研究应侧重于阐明猴痘病毒的生物学和分类学关系,特别是其进化枝,以及它们对疾病的影响。它还强调需要改进 猴痘 的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略,特别是考虑到该病毒具有跨物种 传播 的潜力。

From Sex-Specific Medicine to Xenocortication: A Triangulated Research Brief on Current Frontiers in Medicine and Biology — A Research Brief on Frontier Progress, October 2026

Authors: Dandan Wang
Original abstract (English)
This research brief triangulates two current issues of leading international journals against the review literature that anticipated them. It first summarizes seven articles—three original studies and four reviews—from a themed issue of a leading international science journal (Vol. 6819) devoted to sex-specific and women's health, spanning metabolism, cancer, osteoarthritis, maternal physiology, sex chromosomes, menopause and chronic pain. It then reviews seven original studies from a companion international research issue (Vol. 8135) covering human-mouse xenocortication, sleep-promoting cortical circuits, mTORC1-linked longevity, tumour-draining lymph-node checkpoints, extrachromosomal DNA maintenance and the monkeypox virus replisome. Finally, it reads these fourteen front-line studies against eleven syntheses published in 2023—on proteostasis in retinal ageing, excitotoxicity and neural repair, checkpoint cytology, single-cell annotation, ocular imaging, comparative pharmacology and vaccine hesitancy—showing how each anticipated a theme now at the frontier.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The research brief focuses on the monkeypox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox), MPXV clades, and mpox disease. It explores the biological and taxonomic relationships of the virus, its disease manifestations, and the current frontiers in research related to its mechanisms and implications.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes 14 front-line studies, including 7 original studies and 7 reviews, from two themed issues of leading international science journals (Vol. It also references 11 syntheses published in 2023, covering topics such as proteostasis in retinal ageing, excitotoxicity, neural repair, and vaccine hesitancy, to show how they anticipated current research themes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that the monkeypox virus and its clades (I and II) are central to understanding mpox disease. It emphasizes the importance of studying the virus's biology, including its replisome, and its role in disease progression. The review also notes that the studies anticipated themes now at the frontier, such as the interplay between the virus and host factors.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base shows some heterogeneity in the studies, particularly in the interpretation of the virus's role in disease and its interactions with host systems. There are unresolved questions about the specific mechanisms of the monkeypox virus's replication and the long-term implications of its clades in disease outcomes.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on clarifying the biological and taxonomic relationships of the monkeypox virus, particularly its clades, and their impact on disease. It also highlights the need for improved surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for mpox, especially in light of the virus's potential for cross-species transmission.

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

Published: 2026-10-14  |  Publisher: Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

全球猴痘:现状 - 疾病控制与预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Global Monkeypox: Current Situation - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)关于猴痘的最新情况报告指出,全球范围内猴痘I系和II系病毒仍在持续传播。自2024年11月以来,美国已出现超过90例经实验室确认的I系猴痘病例,这些病例主要与亲密或性接触有关,其中大多数发生在男男性行为者中。对于美国大多数人而言,I系猴痘的风险仍然较低,且这些病例未与死亡相关。自2022年开始的全球II系猴痘传播已导致超过100,000例病例,涉及超过120个国家,死亡率低于0.1%。CDC正在积极监测病例数量、人口统计学特征和疾病严重程度,并对所有样本进行监测检测以确定病毒系别。他们还为有风险人群提供疫苗接种治疗建议,并与公共卫生部门合作,支持预防和临床管理措施。报告强调,I系和II系猴痘在传播、治疗和预防方面具有相似性,并指出美国国内传播的Ib系猴痘可能正在增加。
查看新闻五要素
时间
猴痘 暴发 自 2024 年 11 月以来一直持续存在,截至 2026 年 8 月报告了最近的病例。全球 暴发 于 2022 年开始,自那时起最高的月度病例数出现在 2025 年秋季。
地点与对象
美国和全球 120 多个国家。在美国,至少有 23 个州以及波多黎各报告了病例,其中大多数是同性恋、双性恋或其他男男性行为者。
事件
猴痘 暴发,包括进化枝 I 和进化枝 II,正在进行中。最近的暴发主要通过亲密或性接触传播,有些病例涉及家庭传播。CDC 报告称,进化枝 I 暴发 已归属于 Ib 亚进化枝,而进化枝 II 暴发 自 2022 年以来一直在持续存在。
规模、影响与风险
全球正在流行的 II 支猴痘 暴发 已在 120 多个国家/地区造成超过 100,000 例病例。在美国,自 2024 年 11 月以来,已有 90 多例 I 型猴痘实验室确诊病例。I 型猴痘对美国大多数人造成的风险仍然很低,没有与这些病例相关的死亡,而且大多数人病情较轻。进化枝II的死亡率低于0.1%,而进化枝Ib的死亡率低于0.5%。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 继续监测美国各地的 I 型和 II 型猴痘病例。他们正在进行 监测 测试,以识别发送到其痘病毒实验室的所有样本的进化型。CDC 还为高危人群提供 疫苗接种 建议,并与公共卫生部门合作支持预防和临床管理工作。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Global Monkeypox: Current Situation - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC's current situation report on monkeypox highlights ongoing outbreaks of both clade I and clade II monkeypox globally, with the U.S. experiencing more than 90 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I since November 2024. These cases are primarily linked to intimate or sexual contact, with a majority among men who have sex with men. The risk to most people in the U.S. from clade I remains low, and no deaths have been associated with these cases. The global clade II outbreak, which began in 2022, has caused over 100,000 cases in more than 120 countries, with a death rate of less than 0.1%. CDC is actively monitoring case counts, demographics, and disease severity, and is conducting surveillance testing to identify the clade of all specimens. They also provide vaccination recommendations for at-risk individuals and collaborate with public health departments to support prevention and clinical management efforts. The report emphasizes that clade I and II monkeypox can be spread, treated, and prevented similarly, and that domestic transmission of clade Ib is likely increasing in the U.S.

What to know There are two types of the virus that causes monkeypox, clade I and clade II. Both types can be spread, treated, and prevented in the same ways. Each clade has two subclades (clade Ia and clade Ib; clade IIa and IIb). Recent outbreaks of monkeypox have spread initially through intimate or sexual contact. In some cases, monkeypox was then spread within households. Recent travel-associated clade I monkeypox cases have almost exclusively been attributed to subclade Ib; there have been no deaths associated with these cases and most people had relatively mild illness. The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox that began in 2022 has caused more than 100,000 cases in more than 120 total countries. CDC has vaccination recommendations for people at risk in the United States or who are traveling to countries with clade I outbreaks or new clade II outbreaks. Current situation In the United States Clade I monkeypox The risk posed by the clade I monkeypox outbreak to most people within the United States remains low. In the United States, since November 2024, there have been more than 90 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox in at least 23 states plus Puerto Rico. The majority of these cases are among gay, bisexual, or other men who have sex with men. Many of these cases: Had recently traveled from areas associated with the outbreak in Central and Eastern Africa; Reported sexual contact during travel to parts of Western Europe where newer outbreaks are being reported; OR Reported sexual contact with people who had traveled from these areas. Since August 2026, an increasing proportion of clade I cases in the United States have been reported in individuals with no recent international travel or known links to people who had recently traveled. Cases with presumed domestic transmission have been reported in more than 10 jurisdictions, indicating a likely transition to sustained clade Ib transmission in the United States CDC expects to see additional cases of both travel-related and domestically acquired cases of clade Ib occurring across the United States. CDC continues to monitor both clade II and clade I monkeypox cases across the United States and follow surveillance reports to monitor for any changes in demographics, disease severity, or case counts by jurisdiction. CDC also continues surveillance testing to identify monkeypox clade for all specimens sent to CDC's poxvirus laboratory. Spotlight Jurisdictions can contact poxvirus@cdc.gov for CDC technical support and clinical consultations for severe cases Monday-Friday, 8 a.m.-5 p.m. Clade II monkeypox Clade II monkeypox is still circulating at low levels. Fall 2025 marked the highest monthly case counts since the peak of the outbreak in 2022. Reminder Vaccination is an important tool in stopping the spread of monkeypox. If you have certain risk factors that make you eligible, you can help protect yourself from monkeypox by getting the 2-dose monkeypox vaccine. Monkeypox Vaccination Across the globe Clade I monkeypox Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox in Central and Eastern Africa continue. Beginning in Fall 2025, several countries in Western Europe began reporting clade Ib monkeypox cases among individuals who had no documented history of international travel. These cases were likely related to male-to-male sexual contact. Other travel-associated cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported from other parts of Africa, as well as Asia, Europe, the Middle East, North America, South America, and Australia. Recent monkeypox outbreaks have low death rates During recent outbreaks, death rates ranged from than less than 0.5% (clade Ib) to about 3% (clade Ia). Clade II is the type that caused the ongoing global outbreak that began in 2022. Fewer than 0.1% of people infected with clade II die from the disease. Clade II monkeypox For global case data for clade I and clade II monkeypox since January 1, 2024, see Global Mpox Trends. Confirmed cases include those that are laboratory confirmed as monkeypox virus and may include cases only confirmed as orthopoxvirus. These data are provided for situational awareness and are subject to change. Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks Cases of clade I monkeypox have been diagnosed worldwide. Clade II Monkeypox Outbreaks Clade II monkeypox is spreading in the United States and around the world. What CDC is doing CDC raises awareness for U.S. healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC continues critical work to protect the United States from emerging and infectious diseases. CDC has collaborated closely with key partners in affected countries to help support efforts to stop monkeypox at the source. CDC works closely with U.S. state, tribal, local, and territorial public health departments to provide recommendations for clinical management, diagnosis, and prevention of domestic monkeypox cases. in the U.S. CDC continually increases capacity in communities across the United States for early detection of monkeypox through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing. CDC raises awareness for healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC collaborated with governmental and civil society partners in affected countries to collect and analyze case data, and to identify how monkeypox is spreading. CDC coordinated technical assistance in response to urgent in-country needs, including: increasing the number of monkeypox testing sites; improving specimen transport networks to quickly identify new cases; assisting with Ministry of Health-led vaccine implementation; strengthening emergency management systems; improving case surveillance; and training healthcare workers. CDC's staff throughout Africa provide critical, real-time information to inform U.S. preparedness efforts. In the United States CDC works closely with state, tribal, local, and territorial public health departments to provide recommendations for clinical management, diagnosis, and prevention of monkeypox cases in the U.S. CDC continually increases capacity in communities across the United States for early detection of monkeypox through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing. CDC raises awareness for healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC has information and recommendations for members of the public, including travelers: Destinations | Travelers' Health, Travel Health Notices, and Health Alert Network advisories. CDC continues to work with international partners to help reduce monkeypox transmission. View five news elements
Time
monkeypox outbreak has been ongoing since November 2024, with recent cases reported as of August 2026. The global outbreak began in 2022, and the highest monthly case counts since then were observed in Fall 2025.
Location and population
The United States and over 120 countries globally. In the U.S., cases have been reported in at least 23 states plus Puerto Rico, with a majority among gay, bisexual, or other men who have sex with men.
Event
Monkeypox outbreaks, including both clade I and clade II, are ongoing. Recent outbreaks have primarily spread through intimate or sexual contact, with some cases involving household transmission. The CDC reports that clade I outbreaks have been attributed to subclade Ib, and clade II outbreaks have been ongoing since 2022.
Scale, impact and risk
The ongoing global outbreak of clade II monkeypox has caused more than 100,000 cases in over 120 countries. In the U.S., since November 2024, there have been more than 90 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox. The risk posed by clade I monkeypox to most people in the U.S. remains low, with no deaths associated with these cases and most people experiencing mild illness. Clade II has a death rate of less than 0.1%, while clade Ib has a death rate of less than 0.5%.
Response, status and uncertainty
CDC continues to monitor both clade I and clade II monkeypox cases across the U.S. They are conducting surveillance testing to identify the clade of all specimens sent to their poxvirus laboratory. CDC also provides vaccination recommendations for people at risk and collaborates with public health departments to support prevention and clinical management efforts.

I 型猴痘 暴发 - 疾病控制和预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报CDC 报告详细介绍了自 2023 年底以来在中非和东非持续发生的 I 型猴痘 暴发,截至 2024 年 1 月,全球共有近 60,000 例确诊病例,150 多人死亡。西欧较新的 暴发 可能与这种持续传播有关。在美国,自 2026 年 3 月以来,I 型猴痘病例有所增加,截至 2026 年 10 月,23 个州和波多黎各报告了 90 多例病例。这些病例大多数是男男性行为者,大多数人的风险仍然很低。然而,该群体的风险为低至中等。在美国,暴发 已转变为持续型 Ib 传播,其中许多病例与从中非和东非旅行或与旅行过的人发生性接触有关。高收入国家尚无 Ib 分支病例死亡报告。CDC 正在积极监控 暴发 并为预防、检测和病例管理提供指导,强调态势感知和数据跟踪的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
暴发 于 2023 年底在中非和东非开始,自 2026 年 3 月起在美国的频率有所增加。截至 2026 年 10 月,暴发 仍在进行中。
地点与对象
中非和东非,特别是刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 和美国,至少 23 个州以及波多黎各报告了病例。在美国,大多数病例是同性恋、双性恋或其他男男性行为者。
事件
自 2023 年底以来,I 型猴痘 暴发 已在中非和东非发生,西欧国家较新的 暴发 可能与正在发生的 暴发 有关。在美国,自 2026 年 3 月以来,分支 I 猴痘病例有所增加,在多个司法管辖区观察到持续分支 Ib 传播 的转变。
规模、影响与风险
自2024年1月起,全球已报告近60,000例I系猴痘确诊病例,包括超过150例死亡。在美国,截至2026年10月,已有23个州和波多黎各报告超过90例病例。对美国大多数人群的风险仍然较低,但对于男同性恋者则为低至中等。在高收入国家,尚未报告Ib系猴痘病例的死亡。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 正在监控 暴发,为病例管理、检测和预防提供临床和公共卫生指导。I 型猴痘对美国大多数人造成的风险仍然很低,并且正在通过持续的数据收集和分析来跟踪其传播情况。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC report details ongoing clade I monkeypox outbreaks in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with a global total of nearly 60,000 confirmed cases and over 150 deaths as of January 2024. Newer outbreaks in Western Europe are likely linked to this ongoing spread. In the United States, clade I monkeypox cases have increased since March 2026, with over 90 cases reported in 23 states and Puerto Rico as of October 2026. The majority of these cases are among men who have sex with men, and the risk to most people remains low. However, the risk for this group is low to moderate. The outbreak has transitioned to sustained clade Ib transmission in the U.S., with many cases linked to travel from Central and Eastern Africa or sexual contact with individuals who had traveled. No deaths have been reported in high-income countries for clade Ib cases. CDC is actively monitoring the outbreak and providing guidance for prevention, testing, and case management, emphasizing the importance of situational awareness and data tracking.
